Method: 提出输出分数（损失对最终层输出的梯度）与隐藏层激活之间的条件零均值正交性原理；将该原理统一应用于标准可微损失族；推导交叉熵特例；引入分数向量扩展技术以丰富广播信号；在最优化框架内为三因子学习规则提供理论基础。

Result: 在CIFAR-10和Tiny ImageNet上的实验表明，SBD显著优于现有的广播式信用分配方法，分数向量扩展技术带来进一步的性能提升。

Conclusion: 本文识别了损失分数作为广播信号的核心角色，为神经科学中的三因子学习规则提供了正交性理论和理论基础，并展示分数向量扩展如何丰富目标函数的去相关方向。

Abstract: We introduce Score Broadcast and Decorrelation (SBD), a principled framework for broadcast-based credit assignment for general families of differentiable losses. Error broadcast is a biologically plausible alternative to backpropagation that sends output information to hidden layers without weight transport. The Error Broadcast and Decorrelation (EBD) framework, recently introduced for the mean-squared-error (MSE) setting, grounded this mechanism in the stochastic orthogonality of optimal estimators, under which the optimal residual is orthogonal to functions of the input. We generalize that foundation by introducing an orthogonality principle between the output score (the gradient of loss with respect to the final-layer output) and hidden-layer activations, which holds whenever the optimal score has conditional mean zero. This single principle unifies broadcast-based credit assignment across the standard differentiable-loss families, including cross-entropy, Bregman divergences, proper scoring rules, and exponential-family negative log-likelihoods. The framework supplies a theoretical grounding for the three-factor learning rule under general losses, with the neuromodulatory factor derived as the broadcast loss score. We derive the cross-entropy case explicitly, characterize the admissible loss class, and introduce a score vector expansion technique that enriches the broadcast signal while preserving the orthogonality framework. Experiments on CIFAR-10 and Tiny ImageNet show that SBD substantially improves over existing broadcast approaches, with score vector expansion delivering further gains. Overall, this work identifies the loss score as the signal to broadcast, supplies the orthogonality theory and theoretical grounding for the three-factor learning rule from neuroscience, and shows how score vector expansion enriches the decorrelation directions of the resulting objective.

</details>


### [37] [CSULoRA: Closest Safe Update Low-Rank Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30640)
*Oleksandr Marchenko Breneur,Adelaide Danilov,Aria Nourbakhsh,Salima Lamsiyah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CSULoRA是一种事后修正方法，通过最近安全更新估计来校正已训练的LoRA适配器，在保持任务效用的同时有效降低对抗性微调后的攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全保持LoRA方法依赖投影、剪枝、阈值或额外训练目标等硬干预，可能移除任务相关信息或需要额外调优，缺乏一种既能抑制不安全更新方向又能保留任务相关信息的轻量级后处理方法。

Method: 从安全对齐模型与基础检查点之间的权重位移中估计安全对齐子空间；将每个LoRA更新分解为完全对齐、部分对齐和子空间外三个分量；通过求解闭式惩罚最小变化问题，保留完全对齐分量同时根据相对能量平滑衰减潜在不安全方向。

Result: 在对抗性微调实验中，CSULoRA显著降低了攻击成功率，同时保留了标准LoRA微调所获得的大部分效用增益。

Conclusion: CSULoRA通过闭式最小变化估计实现了安全性与效用之间的有效权衡，为LoRA微调后的模型安全修正提供了一种无需额外训练的高效方案。

Abstract: Low-rank adaptation has become a standard method for parameter-efficient fine-tuning of large language models, but even small amounts of unsafe or adversarial fine-tuning data can substantially weaken the safety behavior of aligned models. Existing safety-preserving LoRA methods often rely on hard interventions such as projection, pruning, thresholding, or additional training objectives. While these methods can suppress unsafe update directions, they may also remove task-relevant information or require extra tuning. We introduce CSULoRA, a post-hoc method for correcting trained LoRA adapters through closest safe update estimation. CSULoRA estimates a safety-aligned subspace from the weight displacement between a safety-aligned model and its corresponding base checkpoint. It then decomposes each LoRA update into fully aligned, partially aligned, and off-subspace components. Instead of discarding components outside the estimated safety subspace, CSULoRA solves a closed-form penalized minimum-change problem that preserves the fully aligned component while smoothly attenuating potentially unsafe directions according to their relative energy. In adversarial fine-tuning experiments, CSULoRA substantially reduces attack success rate while preserving most of the utility gains obtained from standard LoRA fine-tuning.

</details>


### [38] [Diffusion Models Preferentially Memorize Prototypical Examples or: Why Does My Diffusion Model Love Slop?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30642)
*Marta Aparicio Rodriguez,Anastasia Borovykh,Grigorios A. Pavliotis,Daniel J. Korchinski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过Random Hierarchy Model生成的数据训练扩散模型，发现模型优先记忆包含常见子串的样本，而非罕见样本。即使数据集中的样本完全唯一，这种记忆现象仍然存在，说明数据点级去重不能提供有意义的隐私保障。肥尾数据集（含更多非典型样本）会延迟记忆，且这种效应在高层级生产规则引入肥尾时被放大。研究还识别出部分记忆的中间状态，在此状态下常见子串被先学习并在生成时过度产生，若提前停止训练会导致生成的"均值回归"式平庸内容（即"slop"）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型存在记忆训练数据的固有限制，这可能产生法律风险并削弱创造性多样性。理解哪些样本被记忆以及记忆发生的条件是一个重要的开放问题。本文特别关注非典型或罕见样本是否会被优先记忆。

Method: 使用Random Hierarchy Model (RHM) 的生产规则生成字符串数据，在此数据上训练扩散模型，分析模型对不同类型样本的记忆模式。通过控制数据集的结构特征（如常见子串分布、肥尾特性）来观察记忆行为的变化。

Result: 1) 包含常见子串的样本被优先记忆，而非罕见样本；2) 即使数据点完全唯一，该现象依然存在，数据点级去重无效；3) 肥尾数据集延迟记忆，且高层级规则引入肥尾时效应放大；4) 存在部分记忆的中间状态，常见子串先被学习并在生成时过度产生。

Conclusion: 数据集多样性，尤其是高层级抽象层面的多样性，在延缓记忆方面起重要作用。提前停止训练会导致模型生成平庸的"slop"内容。这为理解生成模型的记忆机制和设计更有效的隐私保护策略提供了新视角。

Abstract: Generative models have a persistent limitation: their tendency to memorize training data can create legal liabilities and erode creative diversity. Understanding which samples are memorized in whole or in part, and under what conditions, therefore remains an important open problem. Here we answer the question "Are atypical or rare samples memorized first?" in the negative. We train diffusion models on strings generated according to the production rules of the Random Hierarchy Model (RHM), and find that samples composed of common substrings are preferentially memorized. This holds true even if the training data consists of entirely unique samples, indicating that deduplication at the data point level does not provide a meaningful privacy guarantee. Correspondingly we predict, then observe, delayed memorization for fat-tailed datasets (i.e., those with more atypical samples). This effect is amplified when fat-tails are introduced into high-level production rules. These together suggest that dataset diversity, particularly at higher levels of abstraction, plays an important role in staving off memorization. Finally, we identify an intermediate regime of partial memorization in which common substrings are learned first and subsequently overproduced during generation. If training is stopped in this regime, models will exhibit the reversion-to-the-mean blandness often derided as "slop".

</details>


### [39] [Learning to Perceive the World Through Control: Empowerment-Based Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30656)
*Mahsa Bastankhah,Sophie Broderick,Benjamin Eysenbach*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在强化学习中，通过 empowerment 目标（最大化智能体对环境的影响）学习仅捕捉控制相关特征的表示。分析表明 empowerment 智能体产生前向和后向两种互补表示，两者都对控制无关特征具有不变性，从而隐式学习以控制为中心的世界模型。研究强调通过交互而非被动数据学习表示的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际强化学习环境中，观测维度远高于实际控制所需的变量维度，因此需要学习仅捕捉控制相关特征的表示，以提高学习效率和泛化能力。

Method: 通过 empowerment 最大化目标进行分析，研究其诱导出的前向（forward）和后向（backward）两种表示，并分析这两种表示对控制无关特征的不变性，以及通过交互式学习与因果学习视角的联系。

Result: empowerment 智能体产生两种互补表示（前向和后向），两者都对控制无关特征具有不变性，从而隐式学习以控制为中心的世界模型。

Conclusion: 通过最大化控制影响的交互式学习对于学习有用的不变性表示至关重要，这为强化学习中的表示学习提供了因果学习视角的理论依据。

Abstract: In many practical reinforcement learning environments, observations are far higher-dimensional than the variables that matter for control. In this work, we ask: can we learn representations that capture only control-relevant features of the environment? We study this question through the empowerment objective, which maximizes an agent's influence over the environment and is widely used for unsupervised skill learning. We show that empowerment agents induce two distinct representations -- forward and backward -- that capture complementary aspects of the state, and both of which are invariant to control-irrelevant features. Thus, empowerment maximization leads agents to learn an implicit, control-centric model of the world. Our analysis highlights the importance of learning representations through interaction rather than from passive datasets: interaction aimed at maximizing control is essential for learning useful invariance properties, a perspective that aligns closely with the causal learning literature.

</details>


### [40] [BOKBO (Best of K Bad Options): Calibrated Abstention for VLA Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30660)
*Anya Singh,Cabrel Happi,Jai Relan,Varun Nair,Vidyut Baradwaj*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出BOKBO，首个用于K样本VLA推理的共形 abstention 层，在有限样本下提供分布无关的已执行违规率保证，并揭示了基于扰动的K采样下策略内部非一致性分数的结构性缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时扩展方法（如RoboMonkey、SEAL等）在K个候选动作均不安全时，仍会执行违规动作且无预警，缺乏安全保证机制。

Method: 提出基于共形预测的abstention层BOKBO，包含全局和逐任务（Mondrian）变体；分析并验证了基于扰动的K采样下非一致性分数与安全违规解耦的结构性缺陷；设计基于语义视觉特征和任务标识的学习型违规预测器。

Result: 在libero_object_temp_x0.1上，ε=0.05时条件CRC边界在86%的bootstrap划分上成立，覆盖率为78%，任务成功率为70%；Mondrian-BOKBO将最小逐任务条件保留率从0.71提升至0.93；结果在5个训练种子上稳定，在分布偏移下仍有效。

Conclusion: BOKBO提供了首个VLA推理的有限样本违规率保证，揭示了现有方法的缺陷并提供了有效补救措施。

Abstract: Test-time scaling for vision-language-action (VLA) policies, methods such as RoboMonkey, SEAL, MG-Select, and V-GPS, samples K candidate action chunks at inference and executes the verifier-best. When all K candidates are unsafe, the system executes a violating action with no warning. We propose BOKBO, the first conformal abstention layer for K-sample VLA inference, providing finite-sample distribution-free guarantees on executed-violation rate. We provide both global and per-task (Mondrian) variants, with the per-task variant closing the conditional gap on the hardest tasks.
  Our analysis exposes a structural failure of policy-internal nonconformity scores under perturbation-based K-sampling: the base-policy confidence proxy and K-sample disagreement correlate at 0.98 with the action-noise hyperparameter $σ$, while correlating at the noise floor with actual safety violations. We test the failure's scope by replicating the analysis under token-level temperature sampling and find the failure is mechanism-specific and partially mitigated under policy-stochasticity-based sampling. A learned violation predictor conditioned on semantic visual features and task identity supports tight calibration: at $ε$ = 0.05 on libero_object_temp_x0.1 with OpenVLA-OFT, the conditional CRC bound holds on 86% of bootstrap splits with 78% coverage and 70% net task success. Mondrian-BOKBO raises the minimum per-task conditional hold fraction from 0.71 to 0.93. Results are stable across 5 training seeds, replicate within bootstrap noise on $π_0$-FAST, hold on libero_spatial_temp_x0.1 as a co-equal benchmark, and survive four within-suite distribution shifts. We additionally identify and correct a methodological pitfall: globally-set force thresholds well below expert-typical manipulation forces conflate unsafe behavior with normal manipulation, inflating violation rates by $5\times$.

</details>

Abstract: Many theories of decision making -- planning, reinforcement learning, causal intervention, online learning, and game-theoretic equilibrium -- turn local information into globally coherent behavior. This paper proposes a common categorical formulation: a Universal Decision Learner (UDL) extends a partially specified decision functor from observed contexts to new contexts by a pair of universal constructions. Left Kan extensions express rollout, aggregation, and candidate generation; right Kan extensions express consistency, constraint satisfaction, and fixed-point semantics. The central claim is not that every decision problem has the same algorithm, but that many decision formalisms instantiate the same universal problem: extend local behavioral data canonically, then characterize the globally coherent extensions. We give the abstract UDL construction, prove its universal comparison property, define Kan-invariant behavioral equivalence and minimal abstractions, and show how Bellman equations, planning recursions, causal interventions, online regret, and equilibria arise as special cases. The supplementary material develops the reinforcement-learning specialization in more detail.

</details>

Abstract: This work proposes a context-aware middleware for medical workflow organization and efficiency improvement. In hospitals, laboratories and teleradiology companies, each physician or technician is specialized in a specific kind of diagnosis or analysis. Therefore, certain types of medical images are often forwarded to a certain physician or a certain group. This forwarding is time consuming. That is, repeatedly deciding who would be the best physician, whether he is available at a certain moment given a certain context is exhaustive and may be very inefficient. Thus, the proposed middleware has the ability to process and collect data from images analyzed by each medical staff. Based on the collected data and current clinical context, the middleware is able to infer who would be the best fit staff to receive a certain incoming medical image.

</details>


### [43] [Diversity Matters: Revisiting Test-Time Compute in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30713)
*Yijie Tong,Yifan Hou,Shaobo Cui,Antoine Bosselut,Mrinmaya Sachan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了测试时计算（TTC）策略在视觉-语言模型（VLM）中的应用，发现特征启发式方法无效且投票法增益有限，原因在于预测多样性不足。为此提出基于熵的TTC方法（ETTC），通过预测熵选择最自信的预测，在多模型集成中优于多数投票和最佳单模型，且小模型能协同增强大模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TTC策略已在LLM中展现出提升推理能力的潜力，但在VLM中的应用和收益尚未被充分探索。现有特征启发式方法在VLM上表现不佳，投票法增益有限，需要更有效的集成策略。

Method: 提出Entropy-based TTC (ETTC)：基于预测熵选择最自信的预测。单模型情况下退化为多数投票，但在模型集成中利用置信度差异优先选择更强模型。理论上证明了ETTC在温和假设下优于多数投票。

Result: 在7个VLM和6个基准上的实验表明：ETTC始终优于多数投票和最佳单模型；小模型可以协同增强大模型，实现标准策略无法实现的集成增益。

Conclusion: TTC在VLM上的局限源于预测多样性不足；ETTC通过熵感知的预测选择有效利用多模型集成中的置信度差异，为VLM的测试时计算提供了更优方案。

Abstract: Test-time compute (TTC) strategies have emerged as a lightweight approach to boost reasoning in large language models (LLMs). However, their application and benefits for vision-language models (VLMs) remain underexplored. We present a systematic study of TTC across seven VLMs and six benchmarks, specifically analyzing feature-based scoring and majority voting methods. We find that feature heuristics fail and voting yields only modest gains in single-model settings. We theoretically show that this limitation stems from a lack of prediction diversity: when outputs are highly correlated, voting provides little benefit. In contrast, multi-model ensembles offer richer diversity, yet standard majority voting fails to account for varying model capabilities. To address this, we propose Entropy-based TTC (ETTC), which selects the most confident prediction based on predictive entropy. Our method reduces to majority voting in the single-model case, but in model ensembles, it leverages confidence disparities to prioritize stronger models. We prove that ETTC outperforms majority voting under mild assumptions and empirically demonstrate that it consistently surpasses both voting and the best individual model. Crucially, our results show that smaller models can synergistically enhance larger ones, unlocking ensembling gains not achievable with standard strategies.

</details>

*Stephane Hatgis-Kessell,Emma Brunskill*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究大语言模型能否作为强化学习任务的黑盒策略优化器，提出PromptPO方法，该方法通过向LLM提供状态空间、动作空间和奖励函数的Python描述，并基于 rollout 反馈迭代生成和优化可执行策略。实验表明，在硬探索环境、Meta-World机器人任务和多个真实控制问题中，PromptPO在显著减少环境交互次数的同时，性能往往匹配或超越标准RL基准。LLM输出的策略包括调优的比例控制器、基于规则的规划，甚至运行值迭代等规划算法。但在MuJoCo域中表现不佳，表明LLM策略优化在需要精细连续控制的场景存在局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索大语言模型能否在强化学习任务中替代经典RL算法，作为有效的黑盒策略优化器，即在何种条件下LLM具备足够的策略优化能力。

Method: 提出Prompted Policy Optimization (PromptPO) 方法，通过向LLM提供状态空间、动作空间和奖励函数的Python描述，让LLM生成可执行策略代码；随后根据环境rollout反馈（如奖励累积）迭代优化策略，整个过程无需额外显式提示。

Result: 在硬探索环境、Meta-World机器人任务和多个真实控制问题上，PromptPO在更少环境交互次数下匹配或超越标准RL基线；输出的策略形式多样，包括比例控制器、规则策略乃至值迭代规划算法。但在MuJoCo精细连续控制域中表现不如标准RL基线。

Conclusion: 基于LLM的策略优化在LLM能够利用环境或优化策略的先验知识时足够有效，其优势在于样本效率高；但在需要精细连续控制的场景中存在局限，无法完全替代标准RL算法。

Abstract: We study when large language models (LLMs) can serve as effective black-box policy optimizers for reinforcement learning (RL) tasks, i.e., when can we replace classical RL algorithms with an LLM? We explore this question by introducing Prompted Policy Optimization (PromptPO), an iterative method that prompts an LLM with Python descriptions of the state space, action space, and reward function, then has it generate and refine executable policies based on rollout feedback. Across hard exploration environments, Meta-World robotics tasks, and several real-world control problems, PromptPO often matches or exceeds the performance of standard RL baselines while using substantially fewer environment interactions. To maximize expected return, and without further explicit prompting, the policies PromptPO outputs range from tuned proportional controllers or rule-based plans to policies that run planning algorithms like value iteration. Our results demonstrate that LLM-based policy optimization is sufficient when the LLM can leverage prior knowledge about the environment or optimization strategy. PromptPO underperforms standard RL baselines in MuJoCo domains. This demonstrates possible limitations of LLM-based policy optimization to settings that requiring fine-grained continuous control.

</details>

*Sahaj Raj Malla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Kalimati蔬菜价格指数（KVPI）——一种基于逆波动率加权的复合指数，结合64个因果特征和动量校正在线堆叠集成模型，在尼泊尔农产品价格预测中实现了0.68%的极低MAPE和84.5%的方差解释度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新兴经济体农产品价格因高波动性、频繁供应中断和文化影响而难以预测，现有方法在处理单一商品噪声时效果不佳，亟需一个稳定的宏观信号和鲁棒的预测框架。

Method: 构建KVPI逆波动率加权复合指数（135种商品，10年数据），设计64个因果特征（包含节日领先-滞后效应、滚动统计量等），评估14种模型（统计、树集成、深度学习、混合及Transformer架构），提出动量校正在线堆叠集成模型。

Result: 树集成模型表现稳健，统计模型和Transformer在噪声数据中表现不佳；所提动量校正在线堆叠集成在90天预测窗口取得RMSE为1.771、MAPE为0.68%、R²为0.845的最佳性能。

Conclusion: 该开源管道为尼泊尔及类似市场的政策制定者和供应链参与者提供了实用的价格预测工具，有助于加强粮食安全保障。

Abstract: Forecasting agricultural commodity prices in emerging economies is difficult due to high volatility, frequent supply disruptions, and strong cultural influences on demand. This study introduces the Kalimati Vegetable Price Index (KVPI), a new inverse-volatility weighted composite index that aggregates 135 daily wholesale commodities from Kathmandu over ten years (2013-2023). By creating a stable macro-level signal, the KVPI reduces the noise inherent in modelling individual crops. A rich set of 64 causally valid features was developed, including festival lead-lag effects, rolling statistics, and calendar variables. Fourteen forecasting models spanning statistical, tree-based, deep learning, hybrid, and transformer architectures were rigorously evaluated across short (7-day), medium (14- and 30-day), and long-term (90-day) horizons. Tree-based ensembles proved notably robust, while classical statistical models and complex transformers struggled with the noisy dataset. The proposed Momentum-Corrected Online Stacking Ensemble achieved the strongest performance, yielding a Root Mean Square Error (RMSE) of 1.771, an exceptionally low Mean Absolute Percentage Error (MAPE) of 0.68%, and explaining 84.5% of the variance (R-squared = 0.845) at the 90-day horizon. This open-source pipeline provides policymakers and supply chain actors in Nepal and similar markets with a practical, reliable tool for anticipating price movements and strengthening food security.

</details>


### [46] [Reducing the GPU Memory Bottleneck with Lossless Compression for ML -- Extended](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30728)
*Aditya K Kamath,Arvind Krishnamurthy,Marco Canini,Simon Peter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Invariant Bit Packing (IBP)，一种用于ML训练和推理的无损压缩算法，通过识别和消除张量组中的不变位来减少PCIe数据传输瓶颈，在GNN训练、DLRM嵌入查找和LLM推理中分别实现74%、180%和24%的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ML训练和推理处理的数据集远超GPU显存容量，依赖PCIe进行按需张量传输，造成严重的传输瓶颈。现有有损压缩方法虽能缓解瓶颈，但引入与工作负载相关的精度损失，增加了部署复杂性。

Method: IBP通过识别和消除跨张量组的不变位实现无损压缩；利用GPU优化的解压缩，借助warp并行、低开销位操作和异步PCIe传输提升吞吐量；提供易用的API，可集成到GNN训练、DLRM和LLM推理框架中。

Result: 在GNN训练中平均加速74%，DLRM嵌入查找加速180%，LLM推理加速24%。

Conclusion: IBP作为一种无损压缩方案，避免了有损压缩带来的精度损失和部署复杂性，有效缓解了PCIe传输瓶颈，显著提升了多种ML工作负载的性能。

Abstract: Machine learning (ML) training and inference often process data sets far exceeding GPU memory capacity, forcing them to rely on PCIe for on-demand tensor transfers, causing critical transfer bottlenecks. Lossy compression has been proposed to relieve bottlenecks but introduces workload-dependent accuracy loss, making it complex or even prohibitive to use in existing ML deployments. We explore lossless compression as an alternative that avoids this deployment complexity. We identify where lossless compression can be integrated into ML pipelines while minimizing interference with GPU execution. Based on our findings, we introduce Invariant Bit Packing (IBP), a novel lossless compression algorithm designed to minimize data transfer time for ML. IBP identifies and eliminates invariant bits across groups of tensors, improving throughput through GPU-optimized decompression that leverages warp parallelism, low-overhead bit operations, and asynchronous PCIe transfers. We provide easy-to-use APIs, showcasing them by adding IBP support to GNN training, as well as DLRM and LLM inference frameworks. IBP achieves, on average, 74% faster GNN training, 180% faster DLRM embedding lookup, and 24% faster LLM inference.

</details>


### [47] [OrcaRouter: A Production-Oriented LLM Router with Hybrid Offline-Online Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30736)
*Zhenghua Bao,Fengya Tian,Chris Zhang,Zhenjun Chen,Xile Ma,Yi Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OrcaRouter是一个面向生产环境的LLM路由系统，结合LinUCB上下文赌博机和混合离线-在线学习协议，根据输入请求动态选择最合适的语言模型。离线阶段通过全信息反馈训练岭回归器，在线阶段可选择继续学习。在RouterArena排行榜上排名第二，arena分数72.08，在每千次查询成本1美元下达到75.54%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同大型语言模型在能力和推理成本上存在显著差异，实际部署中面临一个核心问题：对于每个传入请求，应选择哪个模型来处理以在性能与成本之间取得最佳平衡。

Method: OrcaRouter采用基于LinUCB的上下文赌博机方法，结合词法和句子嵌入特征作为上下文表示。采用混合离线-在线学习协议：离线阶段在预训练的路由提示集上评估每个候选模型，获得全信息反馈并构建奖励矩阵，为每个臂（模型）拟合一个岭回归器；部署时从这些参数初始化，并可选择从赌博机反馈中继续在线学习，仅更新所选模型臂的参数。

Result: 在RouterArena排行榜（截至2026年5月20日）上，OrcaRouter-Adaptive以72.08的arena分数排名第二，在每千次查询成本1美元的情况下实现了75.54%的准确率。

Conclusion: OrcaRouter展示了将离线全信息学习与在线赌博机学习相结合在LLM路由任务中的有效性，能够在保持较低推理成本的同时实现高路由准确率，是一种面向实际生产环境的实用解决方案。

Abstract: The rapid development of large language models, each with distinct capabilities and inference costs, raises a practical deployment question: given an incoming request, which model should handle it? We present OrcaRouter, a production-oriented LLM router that combines a LinUCB-based contextual bandit over lexical and sentence-embedding features with a hybrid offline-online learning protocol. Offline, OrcaRouter obtains full-information feedback by evaluating each candidate model on a curated set of routing prompts, yielding a reward matrix used to fit one ridge regressor per arm. At deployment time, it initializes from these parameters and can optionally continue learning from bandit feedback, updating only the selected model's arm after observing its reward. At the time of our RouterArena submission (May 20, 2026), OrcaRouter-Adaptive ranked second on the public RouterArena leaderboard with an arena score of 72.08, achieving 75.54% accuracy at a cost of USD 1.00 per 1,000 queries.

</details>


### [48] [Chain-of-Thought and Compressed Looped Transformers: A Memory-Budget Separation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30757)
*Haozhou Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文比较了三种测试时推理的记忆机制：压缩隐状态循环、完整序列状态循环和思维链暂存器，发现压缩循环的记忆容量受限于循环状态大小，即使增加循环步数也无法解决需要大空间的问题（如P完全问题），而思维链可以通过不断增长的上下文获得更大记忆空间。完整序列状态循环则介于两者之间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究链式思维提示与循环Transformer在测试时计算中的本质区别，特别是持久可变记忆这一核心资源对推理能力的影响。

Method: 通过理论分析和受控实验（指针追踪、联想召回任务），比较三种记忆机制（压缩隐循环、完整序列循环、思维链暂存器）在不同记忆预算下的推理表现。

Result: 压缩循环的记忆容量受限，无法解决P完全问题（在标准复杂度假设下）；完整序列状态循环接近显式暂存器；任务表现取决于持久状态预算是否匹配任务的工作记忆需求。

Conclusion: 持久可变记忆是测试时推理的核心资源，不同记忆机制在表达能力上存在本质差异，记忆预算必须与任务需求匹配。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought prompting and looped Transformers both give a fixed model more test-time computation, but they differ in what they remember. Chain-of-thought stores intermediate state in generated tokens that remain in the context, whereas a looped Transformer carries state through recurrent hidden activations. We argue that this persistent mutable memory is a central resource for test-time reasoning.
  We compare three memory regimes, the compressed latent loop, the full sequence-state loop, and the chain-of-thought scratchpad. Our main result shows that a compressed loop is limited by the size of its recurrent state. Running the loop longer adds computation but does not by itself create a growing scratchpad, so a loop with a small recurrent state remains a small-space reasoner even when run for many steps. Under a standard complexity assumption, such loops cannot decide problems that are P-complete under logspace reductions, whereas polynomial-length chain-of-thought can.
  The separation is specific to compressed loops, as full sequence-state loops carry state at every input position and live in a memory-rich regime closer to explicit scratchpads. Controlled pointer-chasing and associative-recall sweeps illustrate this memory-budget view, with performance sensitive to whether the persistent-state budget matches the task's working-memory demand.

</details>


### [49] [Smaller Models are Natural Explorers for Policy-Level Diversity in GRPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30789)
*Yiming Ren,Yiran Xu,Zicheng Lin,Chufan Shi,Yukang Chen,Dingdong Wang,Tianhe Wu,Junjie Wang,Yujiu Yang,Yu Qiao,Ruihang Chu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出S2L-PO框架，利用同一模型家族中较小模型的固有高策略多样性（pass@k更高）作为自然探索器来训练大模型，通过渐进退火策略从离线小模型rollout过渡到大模型自采样，在数学推理任务上取得显著提升（如AIME 24上+8.8%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO依赖多样化的rollout，但现有方法通过增加token级随机性来提升多样性，这会引入逐步噪声并导致轨迹不连贯。作者发现小模型在策略层面具有更高的多样性（pass@k更高），这种多样性具有时间相关性、逻辑一致性和结构化探索信号的优势，因此希望利用小模型作为天然探索器来训练大模型。

Method: 提出S2L-PO（Small-to-Large Policy Optimization）框架：1) 使用固定的小模型作为自然探索器生成多样化rollout来训练大模型；2) 设计渐进退火策略（progressive annealing），从离线小模型rollout逐步过渡到大学习者自身的采样，以平衡探索与利用，避免小模型容量限制导致的训练中期性能下降。

Result: 在多个数学推理基准上提升准确率，例如使用1.7B探索器引导8B模型在AIME 24上提升8.8%，同时降低了rollout计算量。

Conclusion: 小模型固有的策略级多样性（时间相关、逻辑一致的结构化探索信号）比token级随机噪声更有效；S2L-PO框架通过渐进退火策略成功实现探索与利用的平衡，实现了更快的收敛速度和更高的性能上限。

Abstract: We identify a new dimension for enhancing rollout diversity in Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) for LLMs. While GRPO relies on diverse rollouts, prevailing strategies primarily increase diversity by injecting more token-level randomness, which may introduce step-wise noise and lead to incoherent trajectories. We uncover that smaller models within the same model family inherently exhibit higher policy-level diversity, indicated by their superior pass@k relative to larger counterparts as sample counts increase. Unlike token-level noise, this diversity is temporally correlated, preserves logical consistency, and provides structured exploration signals for gradient estimation. We thus propose S2L-PO (Small-to-Large Policy Optimization), a framework that leverages fixed small models as natural explorers to train larger models. To balance exploration and exploitation, we design a progressive annealing strategy that transitions from offline small-model rollouts to the large learner's own sampling. This shift elegantly avoids mid-training performance drops caused by the small model's capacity limits, achieving faster convergence and unlocking a higher performance ceiling. S2L-PO improves accuracy on diverse mathematical reasoning benchmarks (e.g., +8.8% on AIME 24 using a 1.7B explorer to guide the 8B model) while reducing rollout compute.

</details>


### [50] [Conformal Reliability: A New Evaluation Metric for Conditional Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30807)
*Yachen Gao,Xinwei Sun,Yikai Wang,Ye Shi,Jingya Wang,Jianfeng Feng,Yanwei Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于共形预测的可靠性评分（Reliability Score）作为评估条件生成模型的新指标，并设计了CReL框架来高效计算该评分，实验证明其比现有方法能生成更具信息量的预测集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有条件生成模型评估指标通常仅评估单一输出，无法捕捉生成中的不确定性和潜在风险，缺乏考虑模型固有不确定性的可靠性评估指标。

Method: 基于共形预测（Conformal Prediction）提出可靠性评分（Reliability Score），衡量预测集在预设置信水平下的最差性能；设计CReL框架，包含两步：(i) 构建具有期望覆盖率的预测集；(ii) 在构建的预测集内精确优化可靠性评分。

Result: 提供了覆盖率的理论保证；实验表明该方法比现有方法生成更具信息量的预测集；在合成数据、图像到文本和文本到图像任务上验证了新指标的可解释性及计算框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 基于共形预测的可靠性评分是评估条件生成模型不确定性的有效指标，CReL框架能高效计算该评分，为生成模型的可靠性评估提供了新范式。

Abstract: Conditional generative models have recently achieved remarkable success in various applications. However, a suitable metric for evaluating the reliability of these models, which takes into account their inherent uncertainty, is still lacking. Existing metrics, which typically assess a single output, may fail to capture the variability or potential risks in generation. In this paper, we propose a novel evaluation metric called reliability score based on conformal prediction, which measures the worst-case performance within the prediction set at a pre-specified confidence level. However, computing this score is challenging due to the high-dimensional nature of the output space and the nonconvexity of both the metric function and the prediction set. To efficiently compute this score, we introduce Conformal ReLiability (CReL), a framework that can (i) construct the prediction set with desired coverage; and (ii) accurately optimize the reliability score within the constructed prediction set. We provide theoretical results on coverage and demonstrate empirically that our method produces more informative prediction sets than existing approaches. Experiments on synthetic data and the image-to-text and text-to-image tasks further demonstrate the interpretability of our new metric, and the validity and effectiveness of our computational framework. Source code can be found at https://ggc29.github.io/CReL/.

</details>


### [51] [IRIS: time-structured manifold projections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30810)
*Brian Ondov,Chia-Hsuan Chang,Weipeng Zhou,Xingjian Zhang,Xueqing Peng,Yutong Xie,Huan He,Qiaozhu Mei,Hua Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: IRIS是一种新的流形学习算法，能够在布局中同时考虑时间顺序和流形拓扑结构，适用于动态生物医学数据（如scRNA-seq、比较宏基因组学和文献）的可视化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维生物医学数据（如细胞×基因矩阵）越来越多地以时序方式生成，但现有的流形学习算法（如t-SNE和UMAP）无法在布局中纳入时间顺序，从而模糊了细胞类型或其他类别的动态变化过程。

Method: 提出IRIS算法，该算法在布局构建中同时考虑时间顺序和流形拓扑结构，将两者融合到流形学习的降维过程中。

Result: IRIS能够可视化多种动态生物医学数据，包括单细胞RNA测序（scRNA-seq）、比较宏基因组学以及文献数据。

Conclusion: IRIS作为一种新的流形学习算法，有效解决了现有方法无法处理时间信息的问题，能够更清晰地展示生物数据中的时序动态变化。

Abstract: High-dimensional biomedical data, such as cell-by-gene matrices, are increasingly generated temporally. However, Manifold Learning algorithms, like t-SNE and UMAP, cannot incorporate time-ordering in their layouts, obfuscating the dynamics of cell types or other classes. As a solution, we present IRIS, a new Manifold Learning algorithm that structures layouts both chronologically and by manifold topology. IRIS can visualize a wide range of dynamic biomedical data, including scRNA-seq, comparative metagenomics, and literature.

</details>


### [52] [Learning Permutation-invariant Macroscopic Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30812)
*Zhichao Han,Mengyi Chen,Qianxiao Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种置换不变的自编码器框架，用于从无序微观粒子系统中学习低维宏观动力学，通过重建质量分布而非逐点重建，并在多个粒子系统任务中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维微观系统的宏观动力学建模是跨学科的重要问题，但现有数据驱动方法通常假设输入微观自由度顺序固定，而许多系统（如粒子系统）本质上是无序的，无法直接应用传统自编码器。

Method: 采用置换不变的编码器，解码器不进行逐样本重建，而是重建以观测点为中心的质量分布；联合学习宏观动力学和潜在状态。

Result: 在相互作用粒子系统的能量动力学、Lennard-Jones流体的混合动力学以及聚合物拉伸动力学（视频数据）等多个微观场景中验证了方法的有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 置换不变的自编码器框架能够有效处理无序微观系统，为从粒子系统中学习宏观动力学提供了新思路。

Abstract: Accurately modeling the macroscopic dynamics of high-dimensional microscopic systems is of broad interest across the sciences. Many data-driven approaches learn a low-dimensional latent state through an autoencoder trained for pointwise input reconstruction. These methods typically assume a fixed ordering of microscopic degrees of freedom in the input. However, in many settings, such as particle systems, the microscopic state is inherently unordered. This motivates an autoencoder framework that learns permutation-invariant latent representations. To this end, we adopt a permutation-invariant encoder and design the decoder to reconstruct the mass distribution centered at the observed points rather than per-sample reconstruction. We then jointly learn the macroscopic dynamics of the observables together with the latent states. We demonstrate the effectiveness and robustness of the proposed method across a range of microscopic settings, including learning the energy dynamics in interacting particle systems, predicting mixing dynamics in Lennard-Jones fluids, and modeling the stretching dynamics from video data of polymers moving in an elongational force field.

</details>


### [53] [Unlearning in Diffusion Models: A Unified Framework with KL Divergence and Likelihood Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30825)
*Shervin Khalafi,Alejandro Ribeiro,Dongsheng Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于约束优化的扩散模型遗忘框架，通过KL散度和似然约束实现遗忘与保留之间的最优权衡，并建立了强对偶性理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型遗忘需要在移除不良数据/概念与保持预训练模型效用之间取得平衡，这是两个根本冲突的目标，现有方法缺乏 principled 的理论框架。

Method: 提出三种约束优化问题：基于反向KL散度、前向KL散度和似然约束。利用强对偶性显式刻画最优解为遗忘目标，并设计原始-对偶算法求解。

Result: KL约束方法在保留-遗忘权衡上优于基于权重的基线；基于似然的方法在遗忘效果相当的同时更好地保留了保留概念。

Conclusion: 提出的约束优化框架为扩散模型遗忘提供了理论严谨且实践有效的解决方案，实现了更优的遗忘-保留权衡。

Abstract: Unlearning in diffusion models aims to remove undesirable data or concepts while preserving the utility of pretrained models -- two fundamentally conflicting objectives. We propose a principled constrained optimization framework that formulates unlearning as minimizing the deviation from a pretrained model, subject to explicit separation constraints from the unlearning distributions. Specifically, we formulate three constrained optimization problems based on reverse and forward KL divergences, and likelihood constraints. The first two generalize existing approaches for concept and data unlearning, while the third offers a novel and natural formulation for unlearning. Despite the nonconvexity of the KL constraints, we establish strong duality for all three problems, enabling us to explicitly characterize their optimal solutions as unlearning targets and develop primal-dual algorithms for each formulation. Experimental results demonstrate that our KL-constrained approach achieves superior retention-unlearning tradeoffs compared to weight-based baselines for concept and data unlearning, and that our likelihood-based approach matches unlearning effectiveness while better preserving retained concepts compared to baselines.

</details>


### [54] [Cross-Layer Subspace Coupling for LLM Compression: A Unifying Framework and Its Empirical Limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30836)
*Snigdha Chandan Khilar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现统一SVD压缩方法（如SVD LLM和Basis Sharing）虽然在权重重建误差上提升高达46%，但在下游任务中严重退化。原因在于变压器残差流在前向传播中实际上解耦了相邻层，使得逐层优化比跨层联合优化更重要，因此权重空间重建是跨层压缩的错误目标，未来应聚焦于逐层激活重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一现有基于SVD的大语言模型压缩方法（如SVD LLM和Basis Sharing）到一个优化框架下，并检验其有效性。

Method: 提出统一优化问题框架，在Pythia模型上进行数学证明和实验测试，并通过机制分析解释失败原因。

Result: 统一方法使权重重建误差降低高达46%，但在困惑度和准确率等下游指标上严重劣于标准逐层SVD LLM。

Conclusion: 由于变压器残差流在前向传播中解耦相邻层，权重空间重建作为跨层压缩的目标是有缺陷的，未来研究应聚焦于逐层激活重建。

Abstract: Recent SVD based compression methods for large language models like SVD LLM and Basis Sharing can be unified under one optimization problem. While mathematical proofs and tests on Pythia models show this unified approach improves weight reconstruction error by up to 46% percent it fails in practical tasks. Downstream metrics like perplexity and accuracy severely degrade compared to standard per layer SVD LLM. The authors explain this failure mechanistically. Although the bundle method mathematically couples adjacent layers the transformer residual stream actually decouples them during forward passes. Thus per layer optimality matters more than joint cross layer optimization. The paper concludes that weight space reconstruction is a flawed objective for cross layer compression and future methods must focus on per layer activation reconstruction instead.

</details>


### [55] [CoMem: Context Management with A Decoupled Long-Context Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30842)
*Yuwei Zhang,Chengyu Dong,Shuowei Jin,Changlong Yu,Hejie Cui,Hongye Jin,Xinyang Zhang,Hamed Bonab,Colin Lockard,Jianshu Chen,Zhenyu Shi,Jingbo Shang,Xian Li,Bing Yin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CoMem是一种将记忆管理从智能体主工作流中解耦的框架，通过k步异步流水线使记忆模型的摘要生成与智能体推理并行执行，结合奖励驱动的训练策略，在SWE-Bench-Verified上实现1.4倍延迟改善，同时保持大部分性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的上下文管理方法通过迭代摘要来解决长时域任务，但额外生成的摘要token会显著增加解码开销，影响部署时的端到端响应延迟。

Method: 提出CoMem框架，将记忆管理与智能体主工作流解耦，使其并行执行；设计k步偏移异步流水线，使记忆模型的摘要生成与智能体推理重叠；引入奖励驱动的训练策略，使记忆模型在异步设置下仍能捕获对智能体决策充分的统计信息。

Result: 在SWE-Bench-Verified上，CoMem相比长上下文方案实现1.4倍延迟提升，同时保持大部分性能；延迟增益随系统吞吐量增加而扩展。

Conclusion: CoMem在效率与效果之间实现了更优的权衡，为智能体推理与记忆压缩的独立优化提供了模块化路径。

Abstract: Context management enables agentic models to solve long-horizon tasks through iterative summarization of previous interaction histories. However, this process typically incurs substantial decoding overhead for the extra summarization tokens, which significantly affect the end-to-end response latency at deployment. In this paper, we introduce CoMem, a novel framework that decouples memory management from the primary agent workflow, enabling these processes to execute in parallel. We propose a $k$-step-off asynchronous pipeline that overlaps the memory model's summarization with the agent's inference, effectively masking the latency of context processing. To ensure robustness under this asynchronous setting, we introduce a reward-driven training strategy that aligns the memory model to capture sufficient statistics for the agent's decision-making. Theoretical analysis confirms that CoMem offers a superior efficiency-effectiveness trade-off compared to coupled architectures. Our extensive experimental results on SWE-Bench-Verified show that CoMem provides 1.4x latency improvements upon vanilla long-context solutions while preserving most of the performance. Furthermore, we demonstrate that these latency gains scale favorably with increased system throughput, offering a modular path forward for the independent optimization of agent reasoning and memory compression.

</details>


### [56] [A Lecture Note on Offline RL and IRL, Part II: Foundations of Inverse Reinforcement Learning and Dynamic Discrete Choice Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30843)
*Enoch Hyunwook Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了逆强化学习（IRL）与结构计量经济学中的动态离散选择（DDC）模型在数学上等价，系统梳理了从经典DDC方法（嵌套不动点算法、条件选择概率方法、时序差分方法）到现代ML/IRL方法（对抗IRL、占用匹配、IQ-Learn、离线ML-IRL）的演进脉络，并指出了每种方法的局限（维度灾难、转移核估计、致命三元组、投影不动点偏差），最后介绍了基于经验风险最小化的梯度估计框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连接两个独立发展的学术领域——结构计量经济学中的动态离散选择（DDC）和机器学习中的熵正则化逆强化学习（IRL），证明它们在概率模型上的等价性，并系统梳理各自方法的优劣，为跨领域方法融合与改进提供理论基础。

Method: 1. 证明DDC与熵正则化IRL的模型等价性；2. 梳理经典DDC的识别结果（Magnac & Thesmar）及四种计算范式（嵌套不动点算法、Hotz-Miller条件选择概率法、两种时序差分方法）；3. 梳理现代ML/IRL方法（对抗IRL、占用匹配、IQ-Learn、离线ML-IRL），明确各方法的实际目标函数与识别能力；4. 介绍Kang等的经验风险最小化框架及梯度估计器。

Result: 明确建立了DDC与熵正则化IRL的等价关系，系统揭示了每种方法的核心假设、目标函数、识别能力及局限性，为后续研究者提供了清晰的跨领域方法对照图景。

Conclusion: DDC和IRL本质是同一概率模型的不同表述，经典方法与现代方法各有其适用场景与局限，Kang等人的经验风险最小化框架为离线IRL/DDC提供了一种有前景的梯度估计方案。

Abstract: In the forward reinforcement-learning problem, the reward is fixed and known; the learner is asked to find a good policy or value function. Here we turn the question around. Given offline data generated by an expert, can we recover the reward the expert was optimizing? This is the inverse reinforcement learning problem, and remarkably, two communities, structural econometricians studying dynamic discrete choice (DDC) and machine learners studying entropy-regularized IRL, have been working on exactly the same probabilistic model under different names. We begin by proving their equivalence. We then develop the classical identification result of Magnac and Thesmar and the classical computational paradigms that grew out of it: Rust's nested fixed-point algorithm, the conditional-choice-probability approach of Hotz and Miller, and the two temporal-difference approaches of Adusumilli and Eckardt: linear semi-gradient TD and approximate value iteration. Each route has its limits: dimensionality, transition-kernel estimation, the deadly triad, or projected fixed-point bias. We then walk through the modern ML/IRL strand: adversarial IRL, occupancy matching, IQ-Learn, and offline ML-IRL, deriving each method's actual objective and stating precisely what it does and does not identify. We close with the empirical-risk-minimization framework of Kang et al., which yields a gradient-based estimator for offline IRL/DDC.

</details>


### [57] [DARTS: Distribution-Aware Active Rollout Trajectory Shaping for Accelerating LLM Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30859)
*Yujie Wang,Siwei Chen,Longzan Luo,Xinyi Liu,Xupeng Miao,Fangcheng Fu,Bin Cui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对强化学习中长尾响应分布导致的推理效率瓶颈，本文提出主动分布塑形范式，通过细粒度识别提示内长尾（无效冗余内容），结合分布感知轨迹采样和自适应冗余分配机制，将 rollout 分布向简洁性和确定性方向塑形，在保证模型性能的前提下实现最高1.77倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习系统受限于 rollout 阶段的长尾响应长度分布，导致推理效率瓶颈。已有工作仅通过提示级尾部调度缓解影响，未从根本上解决分布本身的低效问题。

Method: 提出主动分布塑形范式，核心包括：(1) 细粒度识别提示内长尾分布，揭示其由无效冗余内容构成；(2) 分布感知轨迹采样机制，从冗余探索空间中筛选轨迹；(3) 自适应冗余分配方案，同时优化塑形效果与系统效率。

Result: 在多个基准系统上实现最高1.77倍加速，且不牺牲模型性能。

Conclusion: 通过主动塑造 rollout 分布而非被动适应尾部，可从根源上解决长尾分布引发的开销问题，验证了分布塑形在提升 RL 系统效率方面的有效性。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) has become pivotal for improving model capabilities yet suffers from rollout efficiency bottlenecks due to the long-tail response length distribution. While existing works mitigate the impact of long tails via prompt-level tail scheduling, we focus on the root source of inefficiency: the distribution itself. Specifically, we characterize the long-tail distribution at a finer granularity, identifying intra-prompt long tails, and revealing that they frequently consist of ineffective verbosity. To address this, we propose a novel paradigm of active distribution shaping to shape the rollout distribution towards conciseness and certainty, thereby fundamentally resolving tail-induced overheads. We achieve this through a distribution-aware trajectory sampling mechanism, which selects trajectories from a redundant exploration space for each prompt, and an adaptive redundancy allocation scheme to maximize both shaping effectiveness and system efficiency. Experiments demonstrate significant acceleration over state-of-the-art systems by up to 1.77x without compromising model performance.

</details>


### [58] [GlucoFM: A Dual-Stream Foundation Model for Continuous Glucose Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30865)
*Zechen Li,Keerthana Natarajan,Weizhi Zhang,Menglian Zhou,Simon A. Lee,Yuwei Zhang,Maxwell A. Xu,Zeinab Esmaeilpour,Flora D. Salim,Mark Malhotra,Lindsey Sunden,Shwetak Patel,Yuzhe Yang,Ahmed A. Metwally*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GlucoFM是一个轻量级CGM基础模型，通过将血糖数据分解为慢速生理状态流和瞬态事件流，在多个临床预测任务上显著优于现有基线模型，平均PR-AUC提升4.1个点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CGM基础模型将血糖轨迹编码为纠缠的单流序列，未能显式建模血糖动态中不同时间尺度的结构（慢速生理状态与快速瞬态事件），导致代表性学习能力受限。

Method: GlucoFM将不规则CGM记录对齐到24小时时间网格并保留观测掩码，将血糖动态分解为慢速生理状态流和瞬态事件流；采用双重预训练目标：掩码上下文潜变量预测和时间动态预测；在477名受试者的109,066小时无标签数据上预训练。

Result: 在四个不同队列和七个临床预测任务中，GlucoFM线性探测平均PR-AUC比最佳CGM专用基础模型高4.1个点；在所有糖尿病风险和β细胞功能障碍任务及4个胰岛素抵抗任务中的3个上取得最佳PR-AUC；跨数据集迁移性能最优，少样本适应能力强。

Conclusion: 生理感知的分解（慢速状态+瞬态事件）是可迁移CGM表示学习的有效归纳偏置，GlucoFM的分解式架构在核心代谢结局预测上具有显著优势。

Abstract: Continuous glucose monitoring (CGM) provides a dense view of daily metabolic physiology, yet existing generic time-series and CGM-specific foundation models often encode glucose traces as entangled single-stream sequences, leaving the distinct temporal structure of glycemic dynamics only implicitly modeled. We present GlucoFM, a lightweight CGM foundation model that aligns irregular recordings to a 24-hour chronological grid, preserves observation masks, and decomposes glucose dynamics into slow physiological state and transient event streams, capturing low-frequency glycemic baselines and short-term deviations that may reflect acute physiological responses or sensor artifacts. GlucoFM is pretrained on 109,066 hours of unlabeled CGM recordings from 477 subjects with two complementary objectives: masked contextual latent prediction over fused daily representations and temporal dynamics prediction over state and event streams. Across four diverse cohorts and seven clinical prediction tasks, GlucoFM achieves the strongest subject-disjoint linear-probing performance among evaluated baselines, improving average PR-AUC by 4.1 points over the best CGM-specific foundation model. Its gains are most pronounced on core metabolic outcomes, leading PR-AUC on all diabetes-risk and $β$-cell dysfunction tasks and on 3 of 4 insulin-resistance tasks. GlucoFM also achieves the best overall cross-dataset transfer performance and strong few-shot adaptation among evaluated methods, and consistent gains when aggregating multiple days for subject-level prediction, highlighting physiology-aware decomposition as an effective inductive bias for transferable CGM representation learning.

</details>


### [59] [Federated Variational Preference Alignment with Gumbel-Softmax Prior for Personalized User Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30873)
*Jabin Koo,Hoyoung Kim,Minwoo Jang,Jungseul Ok*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FedVPA-GP框架，通过联邦混合先验和正交损失，在去中心化场景下实现大语言模型奖励建模中的用户偏好解耦，有效解决隐私保护下的偏好冲突问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦对齐框架使用单一奖励模型会平均化冲突的用户偏好（如有用性vs无害性），而变分偏好学习在去中心化场景中面临严重的后验坍塌问题，源于局部数据稀疏和异质性。

Method: 提出FedVPA-GP框架，包含：(1) 联邦混合先验，使客户端可将整体人口分布作为动态先验来稳定变分推断；(2) 正交损失，显式强制潜在空间中偏好原型的分离；(3) Gumbel-Softmax先验用于离散偏好建模。

Result: 在HH-RLHF数据集上的实验表明，FedVPA-GP显著优于单一奖励模型基线，成功解耦冲突的用户意图，并支持动态偏好切换。

Conclusion: FedVPA-GP在保护隐私的前提下有效解耦多样化用户偏好，为联邦场景下的个性化大模型对齐提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) offers a privacy-preserving pathway for aligning Large Language Models (LLMs); however, existing frameworks typically enforce a monolithic reward model, inevitably averaging out inherently conflicting user preferences (e.g., helpfulness vs. harmlessness). While Variational Preference Learning (VPL) offers a pathway to personalization, adapting it to decentralized settings presents a fundamental challenge: posterior collapse driven by severe local data scarcity and heterogeneity. In this paper, we propose Federated Variational Preference Alignment with Gumbel-Softmax Prior (FedVPA-GP), a framework designed to disentangle diverse preferences without compromising privacy. To stabilize variational inference, we introduce a Federated Mixture Prior that enables clients to leverage the aggregate population distribution as a dynamic prior. Furthermore, we incorporate an Orthogonal Loss that explicitly enforces the separation of preference prototypes in the latent space. Experiments on the HH-RLHF dataset demonstrate that FedVPA-GP significantly outperforms monolithic baselines, successfully disentangling conflicting user intents and enabling dynamic preference switching.

</details>


### [60] [Zero Collapse: A Failure Mode of Policy Gradient Methods in Discontinuous Reward Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30896)
*Nishant Kumar,Enrique Areyan Viqueira,Amy Greenwald*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了重复拍卖场景中强化学习面临的"零崩溃"失败模式——策略在离散奖励景观中进入零奖励区域后无法有效恢复，并提出了缓解策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重复拍卖中的出价策略是强化学习的重要应用场景，但现有策略梯度和基于价值的方法在处理拍卖特有的不连续"悬崖状"奖励景观时表现不佳，亟需理解并解决这一根本性失败模式。

Method: 通过机制分析揭示离散奖励如何导致梯度信号消失和零崩溃；分析策略随机性与步长之间的交互作用；在REINFORCE和actor-critic变体上进行实证验证，并提出基于初始化和架构选择的缓解策略。

Result: 发现随机探索和基于梯度的更新会导致策略越过最优高奖励区域进入平坦零奖励区域，由于缺乏有效梯度信号，恢复过程极其样本低效；actor-critic方法因有偏价值估计而尤为脆弱。

Conclusion: 提出了拍卖环境的形式化RL框架，揭示了其独特结构特性，并提供了实用的缓解策略以提升训练稳定性。

Abstract: Bidding in repeated auctions is a central challenge for reinforcement learning (RL), combining continuous control with the strategic complexities of digital advertising. While policy gradient and value-based methods seem well-suited for these settings, they often struggle with the discontinuous, "cliff-like" nature of auction reward landscapes. In a first-price auction, for example, a bidder receives zero reward until they cross a specific threshold, after which the reward decreases as the bid increases. This creates a landscape of flat, zero-reward regions separated by sharp boundaries.
  We identify a fundamental failure mode in this setting termed "zero collapse." We show that stochastic exploration and gradient-based updates can cause policies to overshoot optimal high-reward regions and enter flat, zero-reward regimes. Once there, the lack of an informative gradient signal makes recovery extremely sample-inefficient, effectively trapping the agent. We find that actor-critic methods are particularly susceptible, as biased value estimates can accelerate this movement toward unstable regions.
  Our contributions include: (1) a mechanistic explanation of how discontinuous rewards lead to vanishing signals and zero collapse; (2) an analysis of the interaction between policy stochasticity and step size; and (3) an empirical demonstration of this phenomenon across REINFORCE and actor-critic variants. We propose practical mitigation strategies involving initialization and architectural choices to improve stability. Finally, we introduce a formal RL framework for auction environments highlighting their unique structural properties.

</details>


### [61] [Inverse Reinforcement Learning without an Optimal Demonstrator: A Feasible Reward Set Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30903)
*Kihyun Kim,Shripad Deshmukh,Nikos Vlassis,Jiawei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于可行奖励集框架的逆强化学习方法，用于从多个次优程度不同的非完美演示者中学习奖励函数，并提供了理论保证和实用算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统逆强化学习假设演示来自单一最优演示者，但实际应用中数据常来自多个次优程度不同的非完美演示者，现有方法难以处理这种异质性次优性。

Method: 为每个演示者根据其声明的次优水平构建线性约束，形成可行奖励集；通过交集整合多个演示者的可行集；引入函数逼近的离线算法处理高维环境；提出解决奖励模糊性的策略。

Result: 理论证明联合可行集随数据增加单调收缩，刻画了新演示者严格收紧可行集的条件；给出了真实最优演示者可行奖励集的两种恢复保证；在网格世界和LLM微调实验中验证了有效性和理论预测。

Conclusion: 该框架能有效从多个异质次优演示者中学习奖励函数，在理论和实验上均优于基线方法，适用于包括LLM微调在内的实际应用场景。

Abstract: Inverse reinforcement learning (IRL) typically assumes demonstrations from a single optimal demonstrator, but in many applications data come from multiple imperfect demonstrators with heterogeneous suboptimality levels. We study reward learning in this setting through a feasible-reward-set framework: for each demonstrator, we encode its declared suboptimality level as a linear constraint and intersect the resulting feasible sets across demonstrators. Our theoretical analysis shows that the joint feasible set shrinks monotonically as data are added, and we give an exact characterization of when a new demonstrator strictly tightens it. We further establish two recovery guarantees for the feasible reward set of the ground-truth optimal demonstrator: one bound depends on closeness to the optimal occupancy, while the other requires only sufficient coverage and no near-optimal demonstrator. On the practical side, we introduce strategies to address the inherent reward ambiguity in the obtained reward set and provide an offline algorithm with function approximation for high-dimensional environments. Experiments in tabular grid-world and large language model (LLM) fine-tuning settings are consistent with the theoretical predictions and demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed framework over baselines.

</details>


### [62] [Automating Formal Verification with Reinforcement Learning and Recursive Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30914)
*Max Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本论文研究了如何利用验证器环境（通过RLVR和推理时搜索）提升大语言模型生成已验证程序和证明的能力。在Dafny上使用GRPO进行多轮RLVR训练，将验证通过率从9.7%提升至31.1%；在Lean上构建验证器引导的推理支架，结合子目标分解与证明修订器，解决了VERINA数据集中7个先前未解决的任务，并提出了Dalek-Bench基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在自动化形式验证中面临数据稀缺和精确规范匹配的挑战，现有方法仅生成合理代码而非可验证的正确程序。

Method: 1) 在Dafny中使用GRPO及变体进行多轮RLVR训练，组合生成候选程序并用编译器和验证器评分；2) 在Lean中构建验证器引导的推理支架，将证明生成视为子目标分解的结构化搜索，结合验证器反馈、诊断和证明修订。

Result: Dafny上初始验证奖励从2.2%提升至58.1%（后因规范欺骗问题降至31.1%）；Lean支架在VeriCoding上从46.2%提升至69.2%，在VERINA上解决7个先前未解决任务；Dalek-Bench上初步结果较弱。

Conclusion: RLVR和验证器引导的推理搜索能有效提升LLM生成验证程序的能力，但存在规范欺骗问题，且复杂基准（如Dalek-Bench）仍需更强的进展评估和任务特定工具使用策略。

Abstract: Automated formal verification remains challenging for large language models because data for proof assistants and verification-aware languages is scarce, and correctness depends on satisfying precise machine-checkable specifications rather than producing plausible code. This thesis studies how verifier environments can improve LLM generation of verified programs and proofs through reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards (RLVR) and verifier-guided inference-time search. First, we train open-source models in Dafny with RLVR using Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) and related variants, assembling generated candidates into complete programs and scoring them with compiler and verifier outcomes. Initial experiments on an APPS-derived Dafny dataset increased verified reward from 2.2% to 58.1%, but revealed specification hacking, where models exploit weak formal specifications instead of implementing the intended solutions. After filtering underspecified and vulnerable tasks, multi-turn RLVR on the refined benchmark improves the verified pass rate from 9.7% to 31.1%. Second, we develop a verifier-guided inference scaffold in Lean that treats proof generation as structured search over decomposed subgoals, verifier feedback, diagnostics, and repair. With a fixed base model, the full scaffold with proof reviser improves pass rate on an initial VeriCoding pilot set from 46.2% under direct repair to 69.2%. On the larger VERINA dataset, whole-task decomposition plus proof reviser solves 7 of 42 previously unsolved tasks. We also introduce Dalek-Bench, a repository-scale Lean benchmark derived from the Rust $\texttt{curve25519-dalek}$ verification project; preliminary results remain weak, indicating that stronger progress evaluation and task-specific tool-use policies are still needed.

</details>


### [63] [De-attribute to Forget for LLM Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30919)
*Xinyang Lu,Jiabao Pan,Rachael Hwee Ling Sim,See-Kiong Ng,Anthony Kum Hoe Tung,Bryan Kian Hsiang Low*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出首个基于数据归因奖励的LLM遗忘框架DareU，通过强化学习降低模型生成内容与遗忘数据的归因分数，在保持模型效用的同时实现有效遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM遗忘方法依赖最大化遗忘集损失，容易导致过度遗忘和模型效用下降，亟需更平衡的遗忘框架。

Method: 将LLM遗忘的优化目标重新定义为归因归零，提出DareU框架：通过强化学习更新LLM，降低模型生成响应对遗忘数据所有者的归因分数（即去归因），并使用LLM分类器作为归因的高效近似。

Result: 实验表明DareU在平衡遗忘质量与模型效用方面优于现有基线方法，实现了有效的遗忘效果。

Conclusion: 基于数据归因奖励的强化学习方法为LLM遗忘提供了新思路，能更好地平衡遗忘效果与模型性能。

Abstract: The rapid development of large language models (LLMs) has raised concerns on the use of inappropriate data for training, which has led to a growing interest in LLM unlearning. Many existing LLM unlearning approaches rely on optimizing prediction loss(es), such as maximizing the loss on the forget set, but often face critical issues like over-forgetting and poor model utility. To address them, this paper novelly frames the optimization objective for LLM unlearning as one of zeroing out data attribution instead. In particular, we propose the first LLM unlearning framework based on data attribution rewards called DareU that performs reinforcement learning to update the LLM by reducing the attribution score of its generated responses (i.e., de-attributing) to the forget data owners. Empirical evaluation using an LLM classifier as an efficient approximation of attribution shows that DareU outperforms existing baselines by achieving effective unlearning while balancing forget quality and model utility well.

</details>


### [64] [Unsupervised Diffusion Solver for Combinatorial Optimization via Combinatorial Adjoint Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30920)
*Shengyu Feng,Tarun Suresh,Yiming Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Combinatorial Adjoint Matching (CAM)，一种无监督训练框架，将伴随轨迹优化方法扩展到离散组合域，通过连续时间马尔可夫链上的随机控制公式为离散扩散求解器提供结构化且低方差的轨迹级优化信号，在多种组合优化问题上性能优于现有无监督扩散方法，并达到与强监督扩散求解器甚至传统求解器相当的水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的组合优化神经求解器通常依赖大量近优解进行监督训练，这限制了其实际应用。本文旨在消除对监督信号的依赖，将伴随轨迹优化方法扩展到离散组合域，实现无监督训练。

Method: 将扩散型组合优化建模为连续时间马尔可夫链上的随机控制问题，引入离散伴随动力学以传播优化信号，提出Combinatorial Adjoint Matching (CAM)无监督训练框架，为离散扩散求解器提供结构化且低方差的轨迹级优化信号。

Result: CAM在多种组合优化问题上持续优于现有无监督扩散基线，性能与强监督扩散求解器甚至传统求解器竞争。

Conclusion: 无监督训练框架CAM有效消除了对监督信号的依赖，同时保持了与监督方法甚至传统求解器竞争的性能，为扩散模型在组合优化中的实际应用提供了新途径。

Abstract: Diffusion-based neural solvers have shown strong promise for combinatorial optimization (CO), but existing methods typically rely on supervised training with large collections of near-optimal solutions. In this work, we extend adjoint-based trajectory optimization methods to discrete combinatorial domains. We formulate diffusion-based CO as a stochastic control problem over Continuous-Time Markov Chains and introduce discrete adjoint dynamics for propagating optimization signals through discrete generative trajectories. Building on this formulation, we propose Combinatorial Adjoint Matching (CAM), an unsupervised training framework for discrete diffusion solvers with structured and low-variance trajectory-level optimization signals. Empirically, CAM consistently outperforms existing unsupervised diffusion baselines and achieves performance competitive with strong supervised diffusion solvers and even traditional solvers across diverse combinatorial optimization problems. Our code is available at https://github.com/Shengyu-Feng/CAM.

</details>


### [65] [Local linear convergence of gradient methods for overparameterized Gaussian mixtures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30936)
*Jingxing Wang,Vasileios Charisopoulos,Maryam Fazel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究过参数化高斯混合模型的学习问题，提出一种交替使用短梯度步和长Polyak步的方法，克服了过参数化导致的局部收敛速度慢的问题，实现线性收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过参数化在避免虚假局部最优和实现全局恢复方面至关重要，但会显著降低梯度EM算法的局部收敛速度。本文旨在证明慢收敛并非过参数化的固有挑战，可以通过利用损失景观的有利结构来克服。

Method: 提出一种梯度方法，交替执行多个"短"梯度下降步（接近流形）和"长"Polyak步（收缩到最小化器的距离）。Polyak步长在损失景观中具有缓慢增长的流形上能几何级降低损失。

Result: 该方法在混合权重满足一定假设下实现局部线性收敛；对于任意权重的混合，能收敛到近乎最优解（达到自然误设定阈值）。

Conclusion: 过参数化导致的慢收敛并非不可克服，通过设计合适的优化策略（结合短梯度步与长Polyak步）可以恢复线性收敛速率。

Abstract: We study the problem of learning Gaussian mixture models under overparameterization. Prior work has shown that while overparameterization is essential for avoiding spurious local optima and enables global recovery of the ground-truth model using the gradient-EM (expectation-maximization) algorithm, it can dramatically slow down the local rate of convergence. Under certain assumptions on the mixture weights, we show that a standard divergence measure minimized by statistical learning procedures possesses a manifold of slow growth on which the well-known Polyak stepsize reduces the loss geometrically, and design a gradient-based method that converges to minimizers at a locally linear rate. Additionally, we show that our method converges to nearly optimal solutions -- up to a natural misspecification threshold -- for mixtures with arbitrary weights. At a high level, the method alternates between several "short" gradient descent steps that approach the manifold and "long" Polyak steps that contract the distance to minimizers. Our results suggest that slow convergence is not an intrinsic challenge of overparameterization, but can be overcome by exploiting the favorable structure of the loss landscape.

</details>


### [66] [Spectral Anatomy of Quantum Gaussian Process Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30952)
*Jian Xu,Chao Li,Guang Lin,Yuning Qiu,Delu Zeng,John Paisley,Qibin Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示量子高斯过程（QGP）中看似无关的两个现象——HHL回归的指数加速被否定与量子核后验病理——均由同一个量控制：核Gram矩阵的归一化谱熵 $S(K)/\log n$。作者证明了Nyström近似的Cauchy-Schwarz尾界、基于Bach自由度的有限样本方差收缩恒等式，以及目标依赖的最优熵由目标在核特征基下的本征维数表征。实验表明该诊断与核类型无关，且在IBM Heron硬件上实现可迁移，中位绝对误差3.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连接两个看似独立的QGP负面结果（HHL指数加速失效与量子核后验病理），寻找统一的理论解释与诊断工具，并验证其在真实量子硬件上的可迁移性。

Method: 理论推导（归一化谱熵与Nyström近似误差、方差收缩恒等式、目标本征维数的关系）+ 实验验证（多种核族在模拟器与IBM Heron硬件上的熵曲线对比）。

Result: 归一化谱熵统一解释了HHL反去量化、预期校准误差（ECE）和方差收缩三个面板的行为；最优熵依赖于目标平滑性；诊断从模拟器迁移到IBM Heron硬件，中位绝对误差3.2%，且在更大量子比特数（$n_q=6$）上均保持低误差。

Conclusion: 归一化谱熵是量子高斯过程性能的关键诊断量，能够统一预测去量子化可行性、校准质量和方差行为，并可从模拟器可靠迁移到真实量子硬件。

Abstract: Two recent results have reshaped quantum Gaussian processes (QGPs). On the one hand, \citet{lowe2025assessing} rule out the exponential speedups claimed by HHL-based QGP regression in the typical, well-conditioned regime; on the other, an independent line of work shows that highly expressive quantum kernels suffer posterior pathologies that break Bayesian optimization. We show that these seemingly unrelated phenomena are governed by the same quantity: the normalized spectral entropy $S(K)/\log n$ of the kernel Gram matrix. We prove a Cauchy--Schwarz tail bound on Nyström approximation error, a finite-sample variance-contraction identity in terms of Bach's degrees of freedom $d_σ(K)$, and a characterization of the \emph{target-dependent} optimal entropy via the intrinsic dimension of the target in the kernel eigenbasis. Empirically, the diagnostic is kernel-agnostic: hardware-efficient, matchgate, IQP \emph{and} RBF/Matérn/RFF/deep-kernel families all collapse onto identical $S/\log n$ curves on dequantization, ECE, and variance-contraction panels. The NLL sweet spot lives at high entropy for smooth targets and at low entropy for band-limited quantum-data targets. The diagnostic transfers from simulator to IBM Heron hardware with median absolute error $3.2\%$ and mean $5.2\%$ in $S/\log n$ across $24$ configurations at $n_q = 4$, with matchgate and IQP within $5\%$ mean and a single HE configuration returning a $30\%$ outlier that drops to $0.5\%$ on rerun (attributed to calibration drift); the same diagnostic transfers to a second Heron backend (mean error $2.7\%$) and to a $n_q = 6$ scale-up on the original backend (mean error $1.7\%$). No error mitigation is applied throughout.

</details>


### [67] [Revisiting Zeroth-Order Hessian Approximation: A Single-Step Policy Optimization Lens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30960)
*Junbin Qiu,Zhaowei Hong,Renzhe Xu,Yao Shu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ZoVH框架，通过单步策略优化视角统一零阶Hessian估计，并推导出方差最优的完整估计量套件，包括Hessian矩阵、正则化逆及偏置校正的逆Hessian-梯度积，理论分析和实验验证了其优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高维设置中获得低方差的零阶Hessian及其逆估计量仍然是一个重大挑战，现有方法缺乏统一的理论框架。

Method: 通过单步策略优化视角重新诠释零阶Hessian近似，建立通用零阶Hessian估计量与平滑策略优化目标Hessian之间的理论等价性；提出ZoVH套件，包含最优基线方差最小化技术和历史查询重用策略。

Result: 理论证明了Hessian估计量的无偏性、基线的方差最优性，给出了整个ZoVH套件的误差界和曲率感知零阶算法的收敛保证；实验验证了ZoVH在估计精度和收敛性能上的优越性。

Conclusion: ZoVH作为统一的零阶Hessian估计框架，在理论和实践上均优于现有方法，为无导数优化中的曲率信息利用提供了新范式。

Abstract: Accurate Zeroth-Order (ZO) Hessian estimation is a cornerstone of derivative-free methods, essential for tasks such as bilevel optimization, Bayesian inference, and uncertainty quantification. However, obtaining a complete suite of low-variance estimators for the Hessian and its inverse in high-dimensional settings remains a significant challenge. To address this, we propose a unified framework that reinterprets ZO Hessian approximation through the lens of single-step Policy Optimization (PO). This perspective establishes a theoretical equivalence between general ZO Hessian estimators and the Hessian of a smoothed PO objective, unifying distinct classical randomized estimators as specific instances of baseline selection. Building on this foundation, we introduce ZoVH, a comprehensive suite of variance-reduced estimators for the full Hessian matrix, its regularized inverse, and the bias-corrected inverse Hessian-gradient product. ZoVH leverages two key techniques: (1) a unique optimal baseline derived to provably minimize variance, and (2) a query reuse strategy that incorporates historical function queries to enhance sample efficiency without inflating costs. Our rigorous theoretical analysis confirms the unbiasedness of the Hessian estimator, validates the variance optimality of our baseline, provides error bounds for the entire ZoVH suite, and establishes convergence guarantees for the resulting curvature-aware ZO algorithm. Extensive empirical results validate our theoretical findings, demonstrating that ZoVH achieves superior estimation accuracy and convergence performance in real-world applications. Code is available at https://github.com/Qjbtiger/ZoVH

</details>


### [68] [DEM: A Distilled Explanation Model for Interpretable Anomaly Detection in Physiological Sensor Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31007)
*Jyotirmoy Singh,Anushka Roy,Shreea Bose,Chittaranjan Hota*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出蒸馏解释模型（DEM），一种三阶段玻璃盒框架，将梯度提升专家模型的知识蒸馏到基于残差的决策树中，在生理传感器异常检测任务上达到AUC 0.9964，推理速度比SHAP快1235倍，并引入蒸馏保真度指标量化解释可信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生理传感器异常检测方法要么使用黑盒模型（高性能但不可解释），要么依赖SHAP/LIME等事后解释方法（近似而非真实解释），缺乏兼具高精度与固有可解释性的方案。

Method: 三阶段玻璃盒框架：线性基线模型 → XGBoost专家模型学习残差非线性 → 可解释决策树蒸馏专家模型的非线性贡献；引入蒸馏保真度指标衡量解释树对专家模型非线性成分的忠实程度。

Result: 在MIMIC-IV、WESAD、eICU、SmartNet四个数据集上，临床异常检测AUC达0.9964，压力检测AUC达0.9047；推理速度0.17ms/1000样本，比SHAP快1235倍；消融实验证实蒸馏阶段优于朴素残差拟合。

Conclusion: DEM实现了预测即解释，提供用户可控的精度-可解释性权衡，满足生理监测对实时性、高精度和临床可解释性的三重需求。

Abstract: Anomaly detection in physiological sensor data from Wireless Body Area Networks (WBANs) can be caused by sensor faults, network disruptions, or missing data, leading to false alarms. Hence, it demands both high predictive accuracy and clinically interpretable explanations. Existing approaches rely either on black-box models that achieve strong performance but offer no transparency, or on post-prediction explanation methods such as SHAP and LIME. In this paper, we propose the Distilled Explanation Model (DEM), a three-stage glass-box framework that distills the non-linear knowledge of a gradient boosting expert into an interpretable decision tree operating on residuals relative to a linear baseline, so that the explanation is not an approximation but the prediction itself. DEM introduces a novel distillation fidelity metric that quantifies how faithfully the explanation tree captures the expert model's non-linear contribution, providing a principled measure of explanation trustworthiness absent from prior interpretable models. Evaluated across four physiological datasets, including MIMIC-IV, WESAD, eICU, and an in-house SmartNet WBAN corpus, DEM achieves an AUC of 0.9964 on clinical contextual anomaly detection and 0.9047 on wearable stress detection while producing human-readable if-then rules at a controllable depth. Inference requires 0.17ms per 1000 samples, rendering DEM 1235x faster than SHAP-based post-hoc explanation and suitable for real-time physiological monitoring. Ablation studies confirm that the XGBoost distillation step provides measurable gains over naive residual fitting, and depth-sensitivity analysis demonstrates an explicit, user-controlled accuracy-interpretability trade-off unique to DEM among existing intrinsically interpretable models.

</details>


### [69] [An Efficient and Scalable Graph Condensation with Structure-Preserving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31016)
*Yulin Hu,Fuyan Ou,Ye Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种高效且可扩展的图压缩方法SP-ESGC，通过解耦设计分别进行节点压缩和边结构生成，利用热核特征传播、混合聚类和预训练边预测器，实现了高计算效率和跨GNN架构的良好泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图压缩方法存在计算效率低下（耦合优化导致）和跨GNN架构泛化能力差的问题，亟需一种高效且泛化能力强的图压缩方法。

Method: 采用解耦设计，先通过热核特征传播（基于谱图理论的扩散）生成节点表示，再设计混合聚类策略从节点表示中提取判别性类内质心，最后使用预训练边预测器从原始图中推断可迁移的结构模式，生成准确的合成图。

Result: 在真实图数据集上的实验表明，SP-ESGC实现了精确的图压缩，计算效率显著提升，并且在多种GNN架构上具有良好的泛化性能。

Conclusion: SP-ESGC通过解耦节点压缩与图结构生成，有效解决了现有方法计算效率低和跨架构泛化差的问题，为资源受限场景下的GNN部署提供了一种高效可扩展的方案。

Abstract: Graph condensation (GC) is pivotal for enabling Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) deployment in resource-constrained scenarios by compressing large-scale graphs into compact synthetic counterparts. Existing GC methods commonly suffer from computational inefficiency due to coupled optimization as well as encountering poor generalization across GNN architectures. To address these challenges, this study proposes an Efficient and Scalable Graph Condensation with Structure-Preserving (SP-ESGC), which possesses a decoupled design that separates node condensation from graph structure generation. Specifically, it first employs heat kernel feature propagation to generate node representation via spectral graph theory-inspired diffusion. Further, a novel hybrid clustering strategy is designed to extracts discriminative intra-class centroids from the node representation. Finally, a pre-trained edge predictor infers transferable structural patterns from the original graph, ensuring accurate synthetic graph generation. Extensive experiments on real-world graph datasets demonstrate that the proposed SP-ESGC implementes a precise GC with significantly high computational efficiency. Moreover, SP-ESGC also generalizes well across diverse GNN architectures.

</details>


### [70] [Annealed Softmax Greedy in Many-Armed Bayesian Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31034)
*William Overman,Mohsen Bayati*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种无需显式不确定性追踪的强化学习策略（退火Softmax贪心）为何有效。在贝叶斯伯努利多臂赌博机中，当先验满足β-正则性（β=1）且存在大量近最优臂时，该策略可实现接近最优的贝叶斯遗憾率$\tilde{O}(\sqrt{T})$，与经验均值贪心策略相当。这为RLVR/GRPO等不确定性无关策略的有效性提供了结构化类比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR和GRPO等策略优化方法通过采样多个完成并增加高奖励完成概率来更新策略，但这些更新不包含显式的认知不确定性追踪机制。本文旨在研究为何这种忽略不确定性的更新仍然有效，从理论上解释其成功原因。

Method: 采用贝叶斯伯努利多臂赌博机框架，分析退火Softmax（玻尔兹曼）策略。该策略根据经验均值奖励的Softmax选择动作。在先验满足线性上尾条件（β=1的β-正则性）的情况下，证明退火Softmax贪心策略的贝叶斯遗憾界为$\tilde{O}(m + T/m)$，当臂数$m = Θ(\sqrt{T})$时达到$\tilde{O}(\sqrt{T})$。

Result: 在β-正则性下，退火Softmax贪心策略达到近最优的贝叶斯遗憾率$\tilde{O}(\sqrt{T})$；但臂数较少时，相同的Softmax策略可能遭受线性遗憾。该结果为RLVR提供了结构类比：基础策略以非零概率生成正确完成的作用类似于β-正则性。

Conclusion: 当存在大量近最优臂（由先验的β-正则性保证）时，即使不显式建模不确定性，退火Softmax策略也能通过随机采样到其他近最优臂而非明显次优臂来实现有效探索。这解释了RLVR/GRPO等更新为何在没有显式不确定性机制的情况下仍然有效。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) and group-based policy optimization methods such as GRPO update a stochastic policy by sampling multiple completions per prompt and increasing the policy's probability on those with higher reward, regularized by a KL penalty toward a reference policy. These updates do not include explicit mechanisms that track epistemic uncertainty. This paper studies a stylized explanation for why such uncertainty-agnostic updates can nevertheless be effective. We analyze an annealed softmax (Boltzmann) policy that selects actions according to a softmax of empirical mean rewards in a many-armed Bayesian Bernoulli bandit. Under a linear upper-tail condition on the prior (the $β=1$ case of $β$-regularity), which implies an abundance of near-optimal arms, we prove that annealed softmax greedy achieves Bayes regret $\tilde{O}(m + T/m)$, and in particular $\tilde{O}(\sqrt{T})$ when the number of arms scales as $m = Θ(\sqrt{T})$. This is the near-optimal Bayes regret rate in this regime, attained also by empirical-mean greedy. Under $β$-regularity, many arms maintain empirical means close to the optimum throughout learning, so when softmax samples an arm other than the empirically best, that arm tends to be another near-optimal one rather than a clearly inferior one. By contrast, with a small number of arms, the same kind of softmax policy can suffer linear regret. The result also provides a structural analogy to RLVR, where a base policy with a non-negligible probability of producing a correct completion plays the role of $β$-regularity.

</details>


### [71] [UniRTL: Unifying Code and Graph for Robust RTL Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31040)
*Yi Liu,Hongji Zhang,Lei Chen,Mingxuan Yuan,Qiang Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: UniRTL提出多模态预训练框架，通过联合利用RTL代码与控制数据流图（CDFG）学习统一表征，采用互掩码建模和层次化训练策略（包含预训练的图感知分词器、文本-代码对齐、图集成），在性能预测和代码检索任务上持续优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RTL表征学习方法通常依赖单一数据模态（仅代码或仅图结构），限制了表征的表达能力和泛化能力。代码与CDFG分别提供语义/功能信息和完整结构信息，整合这些互补模态对深入理解RTL设计至关重要。

Method: 提出UniRTL多模态预训练框架：(1) 通过互掩码建模实现代码与CDFG的细粒度对齐；(2) 采用层次化训练策略，包括预训练的图感知分词器、文本（功能摘要）与代码的分阶段对齐，以及后续的图集成。

Result: 在性能预测和代码检索两个下游任务、多种设置下，UniRTL一致优于先前方法。

Conclusion: UniRTL通过多模态融合建立了更鲁棒、更强大的RTL表征，为推进硬件设计自动化提供了基础。

Abstract: Developing effective representations for register transfer level (RTL) designs is crucial for accelerating the hardware design workflow. Existing approaches, however, typically rely on a single data modality, either the RTL code or its associated graph-based representation, limiting the expressiveness and generalization ability of the learned representations. For RTL, the control data flow graph (CDFG) offers a comprehensive structural representation that preserves complete information, while the code modality explicitly encodes semantic and functional information. We argue that integrating these complementary modalities is essential for a thorough understanding of RTL designs. To this end, we propose UniRTL, a multimodal pretraining framework that learns unified RTL representations by jointly leveraging code and CDFG. UniRTL achieves fine-grained alignment between code and graph through mutual masked modeling and employs a hierarchical training strategy that incorporates a pretrained graph-aware tokenizer and staged alignment of text (i.e., functional summary) and code prior to graph integration. We evaluate UniRTL on two downstream tasks, performance prediction and code retrieval, under multiple settings. Experimental results show that UniRTL consistently outperforms prior methods, establishing it as a more robust and powerful foundation for advancing hardware design automation.

</details>


### [72] [Learning to Solve and Optimize by Evolving Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31049)
*Veronika Semmelrock,Benedetta Strizzolo,Francesco Zuccato,Gerhard Friedrich,Patrick Rodler,Konstantin Schekotihin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CHECKMATE工具，通过代码进化自动生成算法，仅需形式化规范（what）而无需专家设计求解过程（how），在配置和调度两类工业问题上生成的算法持续超越最先进的求解器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模组合优化问题在工业AI应用中至关重要，但求解通常需要专家进行问题形式化、设计专用求解器和启发式策略，即专家不仅需要指定"解是什么"，还需指定"如何推导解"，过程繁琐且依赖人工。

Method: 提出CHECKMATE工具，采用代码进化范式：形式化规范确保解的正确性并支持系统性能评估，自然语言描述引导进化过程，仅依赖"what"（问题定义）而非"how"（求解方法）来自动生成算法。

Result: 在配置和调度两个工业领域的选定问题上，CHECKMATE进化生成的算法在所有测试案例中均持续超越最先进的求解器。

Conclusion: 形式化方法在引导代码进化以自动解决复杂实际问题方面具有巨大潜力，代表了一种范式转变——消除专家设计求解过程的必要。

Abstract: Combinatorial and optimization problems are fundamental to many industrial AI applications. Solving large-scale real-world instances of such problems typically requires careful problem formalization, specialized solvers, and expert-designed heuristics. Thus, experts need to specify not only what solutions are, but also how they are derived. By introducing the tool CHECKMATE, we show that algorithm generation via code evolution represents a paradigm shift by eliminating the need to formulate the how. CHECKMATE solely relies on the what. Specifically, a formal specification ensures solutions' correctness and enables systematic performance evaluation of the generated programs, while a natural language description guides the evolutionary process. The effectiveness of our method is demonstrated on selected problems from two industrial domains: configuration and scheduling. In all cases, the evolved algorithms consistently outperform state-of-the-art solvers. This underscores the potential of formal methods in guiding code evolution for automatically solving complex real-world problems.

</details>


### [73] [Best-Arm Identification-Based Trust Region Selection for Bayesian Optimization on Multimodal Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31050)
*Nobuo Namura,Sho Takemori*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种轨迹感知的贝叶斯优化框架，结合最佳臂识别（BAI）与信任区域方法，通过外推多个局部优化器的轨迹并逐步淘汰次优候选，加速多模态优化问题的全局收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统高斯过程贝叶斯优化在处理复杂多模态或高维问题时性能下降，虽然信任区域BO通过聚焦局部区域缓解了该问题，但如何有效选择最优区域仍是一个挑战，最新研究将其建模为多臂老虎机问题。

Method: 提出轨迹感知框架，将最佳臂识别（BAI）与信任区域BO相结合。具体地，外推多个局部初始化优化器的优化轨迹以预测其最终性能，并通过BAI逐步淘汰次优候选，从而聚焦最有潜力的区域。

Result: 理论证明在温和假设下，所提的BAI引导的BO比传统BO更快收敛到全局最优；在合成基准和真实世界基准上的大量实验验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: 轨迹感知结合BAI的信任区域BO方法能有效提升多模态优化问题的求解效率，在收敛速度和全局最优性方面优于传统BO方法。

Abstract: Gaussian process-based Bayesian optimization (BO) is a popular approach for expensive black-box optimization, but its performance often degrades on complex multimodal or high-dimensional problems. Trust region-based BO mitigates this issue by focusing on local regions, and recent studies suggest that selecting an effective region can be formulated as a multi-armed bandit problem. We propose a trajectory-aware framework that integrates best-arm identification (BAI) with trust region-based BO to efficiently solve multimodal optimization problems. Our method extrapolates the optimization trajectories of multiple locally initialized optimizers to predict their final performance and progressively eliminates suboptimal candidates via BAI. We theoretically show that the proposed BAI-guided BO converges faster to the global optimum than conventional BO under mild assumptions, and demonstrate its effectiveness through extensive experiments on synthetic and real-world benchmarks.

</details>


### [74] [Learning to Bid in FCR Markets: A Best-of-Both-Worlds Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31070)
*Marius Potfer,Cheng Wan,Pierre Gruet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对欧洲频率 containment 备用（FCR）市场的竞价问题，提出将多国FCR出清问题重构为重复多单元统一价格拍卖，并采用Best-of-Both-Worlds组合半赌博机算法实现在线学习，在随机环境下达到对数伪遗憾，在对抗环境下达到O(√T)遗憾，回测显示稳定产品上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FCR市场中竞争报价隐藏，投标者只能观察到部分市场反馈（如出清价格和成交数量），这使得灵活供应商的竞价决策极具挑战性。现有方法未能充分利用可观测的市场反馈信息进行学习优化。

Method: 将多国FCR出清问题重构为重复多单元统一价格拍卖，并转化为在线学习问题；采用Best-of-Both-Worlds组合半赌博机算法，仅利用标准市场反馈（出清价格和成交数量）即可实现学习与竞价优化。

Result: 在随机环境下实现对数伪遗憾，在对抗环境下实现O(√T)遗憾；合成实验验证了理论缩放性；历史欧洲FCR数据回测显示，该方法在稳定产品上表现优异，而EXP3类基线在强非平稳条件下更安全。

Conclusion: 基于学习的FCR市场竞价方法在理论上具有扎实基础，实践中当学习规则与产品级市场稳定性匹配时具有实用价值。

Abstract: Bidding in the European Frequency Containment Reserve (FCR) market is challenging for flexibility providers because competing offers are hidden and bidders observe only partial feedback form the market, such as, clearing price and awarded quantity. For a participant active in a single country, we show that the multi-country FCR clearing problem can be recast as a repeated multi-unit uniform-price auction against an endogenous vector of opposing bids. This reformulation yields an online learning problem and allows us to adapt a Best-of-Both-Worlds combinatorial semi-bandit algorithm implementable from this standard market feedback. The resulting bidder achieves logarithmic pseudo-regret in stochastic environments and $\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{T})$ regret in adversarial ones. Synthetic experiments confirm the expected scaling, and backtests on historical European FCR data show competitive performance in practice: the method performs especially well on stable products, while EXP3-type baselines can be safer under stronger non-stationarity. Overall, the results show that learning-based bidding in FCR markets is theoretically grounded and practically useful when the learning rule matches product-level market stability.

</details>


### [75] [Subspace-Decomposed JEPAs: Disentangling Progression and Content in Latent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31111)
*Lucas Thil,Jesse Read,Rim Kaddah,Guillaume Doquet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SD-JEPA，将联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）的潜在空间分解为正交的进展子空间（低维，由余弦三元组损失塑造）和内容子空间（高维，由SIGReg正则化）。证明了两种抗坍缩力作用于不相交坐标，实现加性组合。SD-JEPA在多数控制基准上优于LeWM基线，且1D角度进展坐标可作为场景感知罗盘，在回溯、扰动下表现出语义合理的重定位，优于标准预测误差的惊奇检测（AUROC提升最高+0.18，每集胜率97.5%），8维进展子空间（占潜在4.2%）解释72-95%任务进展方差。


<details>
