Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) are bottlenecked by sparse, irregular memory access. Popular frameworks such as DGL and PyTorch Geometric support general message passing, but complex layers often materialize edge-wise intermediates, increasing memory traffic and limiting scalability on large graphs. We take an I/O- and arithmetic-intensity--centric view and show that widely used layers fall into three kernel families: SpMM-based convolutions, reduction-based aggregations, and attention-based layers (GATv2/Graph Transformer). For each family, we develop GPU kernels that reduce data movement, improve locality, and remain robust across realistic graphs. We also study graph reordering and find that its impact depends on the kernel mapping: it benefits neighbor-parallel (gather-dominated) kernels more consistently than feature-parallel designs. Empirically, our fused attention kernels reach up to $\textbf{3.9}\times$ speedup for Graph Transformer (median $\textbf{1.6}\times$), with Tensor Core (block-sparse) variants up to $\textbf{7.3}\times$ on locally dense graphs; for GATv2 we reach up to $\textbf{8.5}\times$ speedup (median $\textbf{2.0}\times$) while reducing peak memory by up to $\textbf{76}\times$ (median $\textbf{6}\times$). Our degree-aware reduction kernels achieve up to $\textbf{10}\times$ speedup (median $\textbf{2.6}\times$). For SpMM-based layers, properly cached cuSPARSE achieves up to $\textbf{8}\times$ speedup over DGL and outperforms evaluated custom baselines in the majority of evaluations. We release our implementations as drop-in replacements to support reproducible, hardware-aware GNN acceleration.

</details>


### [115] [When Are Multimodal Predictions Biologically Supported? A Diagnostic Evaluation Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31504)
*Dylan Steiner,Gustavo Arango-Argoty,Gerald Sun,Etai Jacob*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DECAT是一个模型无关的事后评估框架，能够将多模态表征分类为四种诊断场景，有效区分共享生物学、单模态生物学和虚假关联，在合成数据（2500+表征）和TCGA真实数据（8979名患者）上验证发现，纠缠模型（如CLIP）能完美检测共享生物学但假阳性率高，且该错误率随混杂强度增加。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态肿瘤模型虽能产生准确预测，但无法揭示模型学到的是跨模态共享的生物学、单模态特有的生物学，还是反映混杂因素而非真实生物学的虚假关联。现有方法无法有效区分这些情况。

Method: 提出DECAT框架，通过五个零假设参照指标和基于规则的决策程序，将多模态表征分类为四种诊断场景。该框架基于学习到的表征运行，无需知道特定混杂因素的存在，证据不足时返回不确定结果。

Result: 在合成数据上验证了四种多模态模型类（2500+训练表征），在TCGA真实数据上评估了多模态嵌入和五个预训练病理基础模型。发现CLIP等纠缠模型能完美检测共享生物学，但在共享生物学不存在时大多数情况下会错误声称存在；该错误率随混杂强度增加；DECAT能检测到AUROC无法发现的混杂。

Conclusion: DECAT能够有效区分多模态表征中的共享生物学、单模态生物学和虚假关联，无需事先知道混杂因素标签即可检测到AUROC不可见的混杂，为多模态模型评估提供了更可靠的诊断工具。

Abstract: Multimodal models in oncology can produce accurate predictions, but accurate prediction does not reveal whether the model has learned biology that is shared across modalities, biology confined to one modality, or spurious correlations that reflect confounders rather than genuine biology. We introduce DECAT, a model-agnostic post-hoc evaluation framework that classifies multimodal representations into four diagnostic scenarios for a given task and modality, using five null-referenced metrics and a rule-based decision procedure. The framework operates on learned representations, requires no knowledge of which specific confounder is present, and returns indeterminate when the evidence is insufficient. We validate DECAT on synthetic data across four multimodal model classes (over 2,500 trained representations) and on real data from 8,979 TCGA patients, evaluating both multimodal embeddings and five pretrained pathology foundation models. Entangled models (e.g., CLIP) achieve near-perfect shared biology detection but falsely claim shared biology in the majority of cases where it is absent on real foundation model embeddings. This false claim rate increases with confound strength so that larger cohorts and stronger representations produce more confident but still incorrect diagnoses. Applied to both multimodal TCGA embeddings and five pathology foundation models without paired RNA, DECAT detects confounding invisible to AUROC without requiring the confounder labels, as confirmed by post-hoc stratification.

</details>


### [116] [Skill Reuse as Compression in Agentic RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31509)
*Zhikun Xu,Yu Feng,Jacob Dineen,Taiwei Shi,Jieyu Zhao,Ben Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ReuseRL，基于最小描述长度（MDL）原则，通过从成功轨迹中提取共享技能字典并引入分割代价来惩罚编码效率低的特殊行为，从而提升强化学习智能体的泛化能力。在ALFWorld等任务中，ReuseRL在分布内和分布外场景均优于GRPO等基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准RL训练的大语言模型智能体往往学到脆弱、特定于任务的捷径，泛化能力差。作者假设当成功轨迹在结构上可压缩（即可分解为少量可复用的抽象模式）时，智能体能更好地泛化。

Method: 基于MDL原则的ReuseRL框架：1）从成功轨迹中提取共享技能字典；2）在RL目标中引入分割代价，显式惩罚编码效率低的特殊行为；3）证明了该压缩惩罚的PAC-Bayes泛化界。

Result: 在ALFWorld、TextWorld-Cooking和Countdown-Stepwise三个任务上，ReuseRL在分布内和分布外场景的成功率均优于vanilla GRPO和强round-length基线。

Conclusion: 通过MDL原则对成功轨迹进行结构化压缩，能够有效提升LLM智能体的泛化能力，避免过拟合到任务特定捷径。

Abstract: Large language model agents trained with reinforcement learning (RL) often learn brittle, task-specific shortcuts. We hypothesize that agents generalize better when their successful trajectories are structurally compressible, decomposed into a small set of reusable abstract patterns. To formalize this, we introduce ReuseRL, which grounds agentic RL in the Minimum Description Length (MDL) principle. ReuseRL extracts a shared skill dictionary from successful trajectories and augments the RL objective with a segmentation cost, explicitly penalizing idiosyncratic behaviors that encode poorly. We prove a PAC-Bayes generalization bound for this compression penalty. Across ALFWorld, TextWorld-Cooking, and Countdown-Stepwise, ReuseRL improves in- and out-of-distribution success over vanilla GRPO and strong round-length baselines.

</details>


### [117] [Chem-PerturBridge: a harmonized compendium of small molecule perturbation transcriptomic effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31522)
*Artur Szałata,Olga Novitskaia,Maiia Shulman,Matthew Mella,Altynbek Zhubanchaliyev,Fabian J. Theis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Chem-PerturBridge是一个包含超过3.7万种化合物、136种细胞环境和125万个转录组样本的跨数据集资源，用于评估跨数据集扰动签名一致性及预训练化合物表示模型。研究发现跨数据集的logFC排名和幅度一致性较弱，但方向一致性较好；基于该资源的预训练嵌入在多个基准上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有用于小分子建模的转录组资源在技术平台、元数据标准、对照设置、剂量和处理流程等方面高度碎片化，缺乏统一的、多样化的训练数据资源来支撑大规模扰动模型。

Method: 整合多数据集构建Chem-PerturBridge资源（覆盖37k+化合物、136种细胞背景、8种检测类型、125万样本），使用标准化标识符和元数据，计算条件水平效应；采用两种评估策略：跨数据集配对条件一致性分析（logFC排名、幅度和方向一致性）和化合物表示学习的预训练评估（OP3分裂和11数据集分子留出评估）。

Result: 相同化合物的配对条件在跨数据集间logFC排名和幅度一致性普遍较弱（低于相同背景不同化合物的基线），但方向一致性较高且超过基线；基于Chem-PerturBridge预训练的嵌入在化合物留出评估中优于L1000-only嵌入、Morgan指纹和OP3基线，在11个数据集留出评估中表现最优或持平。

Conclusion: Chem-PerturBridge既可作为跨数据集签名一致性的诊断评估工具，也可作为异质性扰动转录组数据模型导向复用的预训练资源，支持化合物表示学习性能提升。

Abstract: Large perturbation models require training data encompassing chemical, cellular, and assay diversity. Current transcriptomic resources for small-molecule modeling, however, are fragmented across technologies, metadata conventions, controls, doses, and preprocessing pipelines. We introduce Chem-PerturBridge, a harmonized multi-dataset resource comprising over 37k compounds, 136 cellular contexts, and 1.25M transcriptomic samples across eight assay types, with standardized identifiers, metadata, and replicate-aware condition-level effects. We use the resource to evaluate matched-condition agreement across datasets and replicate agreement within datasets. Matched same-compound conditions generally show weak agreement in fine-grained logFC rankings and magnitudes across most dataset pairs, often falling below same-context different-compound baselines. In contrast, logFC direction agreement is substantially more stable and usually exceeds these baselines. We further evaluate Chem-PerturBridge as a pretraining resource for compound representation learning. Under a compound-held-out OP3 evaluation split, embeddings pretrained on Chem-PerturBridge improve over L1000-only embeddings, Morgan fingerprints, and the descriptor-free OP3 baseline across metrics. An extensive molecule-holdout evaluation across 11 datasets further shows that models trained on Chem-PerturBridge outperform or match those that are not. Chem-PerturBridge therefore supports both diagnostic evaluation of cross-dataset signature agreement and model-oriented reuse of heterogeneous perturbation transcriptomic data.

</details>


### [118] [Value Functions as Supermartingale Certificates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31524)
*Alessandro Abate,Daniel Contro,Mirco Giacobbe,Agustín Martínez-Suñé,Diptarko Roy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了强化学习与形式化验证之间的理论联系：在适当奖励函数下，几乎必然满足ω-正则性质策略的价值函数编码了Streett超鞅证书，为通过RL进行证书合成提供了理论依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有随机系统的形式化验证方法（基于超鞅证书）能提供几乎必然满足ω-正则性质的充分证明规则，但缺乏自动合成证书的手段；而强化学习方法虽然能处理ω-正则任务，但通常缺乏形式化保证。作者旨在弥合这两条研究路线之间的鸿沟。

Method: 建立理论连接：证明在适当设计的奖励函数下，几乎必然满足ω-正则性质的策略所对应的值函数编码了一个Streett超鞅证书。该结论在有限、可数无限和连续状态空间上均成立，并通过有限马尔可夫决策过程的实验进行验证。

Result: 理论上建立了值函数与Streett超鞅证书之间的等价关系；实验上在有限MDP上验证了该理论连接的可行性。

Conclusion: 该理论连接为通过强化学习合成形式化验证证书提供了一条有原则的途径，使得RL方法在保证ω-正则性质满足性的同时，能够自动生成可验证的证书。

Abstract: Certification methods for stochastic systems provide sufficient proof rules, based on real-valued supermartingale certificates, to determine the almost-sure satisfaction of $ω$-regular properties (and therefore of linear temporal logic) over general state spaces, encompassing both countably infinite and continuous state spaces. Conversely, reinforcement learning (RL) methods for $ω$-regular tasks have received considerable attention, but they typically lack formal guarantees that the learned policy satisfies the specification, except possibly for finite state and action spaces. We bridge these two lines of research by establishing a novel theoretical connection: under an appropriate reward, the value function associated to a policy that almost surely satisfies an $ω$-regular property encodes a Streett supermartingale certificate for that specification. Our results, validated experimentally on finite Markov decision processes, hold for finite, countably infinite, and continuous state spaces, suggesting a principled route to certificate synthesis via RL.

</details>


### [119] [The Dynamic-Probabilistic Consistency Gap in Chaotic Surrogate Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31547)
*Andre Herz,Matthijs Pals,Daniel Durstewitz,Georgia Koppe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了动力系统重构中的动态-概率一致性差距（DPC gap），即有限时域概率目标会损害动力学特性或使预测不确定性与局部切线动力学解耦，并提出了基于可微扩展卡尔曼滤波的KAFFEE框架来缓解这一问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的动力系统重构方法在追求有限时域概率目标时，会导致动态-概率一致性差距：要么退化系统的动力学特性，要么使预测不确定性与局部切线动力学（即不确定性应反映的物理扩展行为）脱钩。

Method: 提出KAFFEE（Kalman-Aware Framework For Ergodic Emulation）框架，基于可微扩展卡尔曼滤波进行训练，通过评估局部预测残差（创新）的似然，同时通过学习到的局部雅可比矩阵传递协方差。

Result: 在随机超混沌Lorenz-96系统上，KAFFEE减少了识别出的失效模式，改善了动力学不变量重构，并保持了竞争性的预测分数。在13个混沌系统上，KAFFEE实现了上下文贝叶斯滤波，同时基本保留了零样本动力学特性。

Conclusion: 开放环高斯展开目标会惩罚混沌系统中雅可比生成的协方差增长，鼓励弱化物理解扩或使不确定性与之解耦的优化捷径；KAFFEE通过卡尔曼感知训练框架有效缓解了这一问题。

Abstract: Dynamical systems reconstruction (DSR) aims to learn surrogate models that capture the dynamics underlying time-series data. Reliably deploying these surrogates requires uncertainty estimates consistent with the learned dynamics. We expose a dynamic-probabilistic consistency (DPC) gap: the pursuit of finite-horizon probabilistic objectives can degrade dynamics or decouple predictive uncertainty from the local tangent dynamics it ought to reflect. We isolate three mechanisms behind this gap: core collapse, noise masking, and blind uncertainty. Specifically, we show that open-loop Gaussian rollout objectives can penalize Jacobian-generated covariance growth in chaotic systems, encouraging optimization shortcuts that weaken physical expansion or decouple uncertainty from it. To mitigate this gap, we propose KAFFEE (Kalman-Aware Framework For Ergodic Emulation), a differentiable extended Kalman filter-based training framework that evaluates likelihood on local predictive residuals (innovations) while transporting covariance through learned local Jacobians. On stochastic hyperchaotic Lorenz-96, KAFFEE reduces the identified failure modes, improves reconstruction of dynamical invariants relative to open-loop objectives, and maintains competitive predictive scores. We further show that the DPC gap appears when probabilistically adapting a DSR foundation model across 13 chaotic systems, where KAFFEE enables in-context Bayesian filtering while largely preserving zero-shot dynamics.

</details>


### [120] [Positional versus Symbolic Attention Heads: Learning Dynamics, RoPE Geometry, and Length Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31558)
*Felipe Urrutia,Juan José Alegría,Cinthia Sanchez Macias,Jorge Salas,Cristian B. Calderon,Cristobal Rojas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过研究GPT-J在两种结构等价的多跳推理任务（数字位置推理和字母符号推理）上的学习动态，发现成功学习与纯注意力头（纯位置头或纯符号头）的出现相关。尽管任务结构等价，但数字任务需要位置头和符号头，而字母任务仅需符号头。通过理论构建和实证分析，揭示了符号机制在长序列外推上比位置机制更可靠，并形式化了二者之间的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Transformer模型解决结构化任务的机制，以及如何预测其在未见场景中的行为，对于安全部署至关重要。

Method: 在两种结构等价的多跳推理任务（数字位置推理和字母符号推理）上训练单层解码器Transformer（GPT-J），利用分类注意力头行为的指标（位置型vs符号型），结合理论构建（RoPE注意力）和实证验证。

Result: 成功学习与纯注意力头的出现相关；数字任务需要位置头和符号头，字母任务仅需符号头；符号机制对长序列外推更可靠，位置机制面临更严格的局限性。

Conclusion: 位置机制和符号机制在长序列鲁棒性上存在定量分离，符号机制具有更好的外推能力。

Abstract: Transformer-based language models are widespread in today's society. As such, understanding the mechanisms by which they solve structured tasks and predicting how they may behave in novel scenarios is of great importance for safe deployment. We study the learning dynamics of attention heads in a controlled setting by training a decoder-only Transformer (GPT-J) on two structurally equivalent multi-hop reasoning tasks: a number task requiring positional reasoning and a letter task requiring symbolic reasoning. Using a recently introduced metric that classifies attention-head behavior as positional or symbolic for a given prompt, we show that successful learning is associated with the emergence of pure heads, i.e., heads that express themselves as either positional or symbolic. Despite the tasks' structural equivalence, they impose different mechanistic demands: the number task requires both positional and symbolic heads, whereas the letter task requires only symbolic heads. We then identify the computational roles of these heads, characterize the basic functions they implement, and give theoretical constructions showing how single-layer RoPE-based attention can realize these functions through geometrically interpretable query, key, and value operations. This analysis yields a quantitative separation between positional and symbolic mechanisms in their robustness to longer sequences, formalized through a novel notion of discrepancy. We empirically validate the resulting predictions in both controlled and real-world models, showing that symbolic mechanisms extrapolate more reliably to longer sequences while positional mechanisms face sharper limitations.

</details>


### [121] [Functional Attention: From Pairwise Affinities to Functional Correspondences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31559)
*Jiefang Xiao,Maolin Gao,Simon Weber,Guandao Yang,Daniel Cremers*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Functional Attention，通过将注意力机制重新解释为自适应基函数之间的函数对应关系，用结构化线性算子替代softmax亲和度，从而构建紧凑、泛化性强、分辨率不变的算子学习方法，在PDE求解、3D分割和回归等任务上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于Transformer的算子学习方法将连续场离散化为独立token进行处理，依赖逐点注意力机制，忽略了连续函数空间的全局结构信息，导致表示能力受限且对离散化方式敏感。

Method: 受几何函数映射的启发，将注意力机制重新解释为自适应基之间的函数对应关系，用结构化的线性算子替代传统的softmax亲和度矩阵，从而显式捕获全局依赖关系。

Result: 在多个算子学习任务（包括PDE求解、3D分割和回归）上达到与现有SOTA方法相当的性能，同时对不同的离散化方式具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: Functional Attention提供了一种紧凑、泛化性强且分辨率不变的算子学习范式，通过将注意力机制从离散token匹配提升为连续函数空间中的基对应，有效解决了现有方法忽视全局功能结构的问题。

Abstract: Learning mappings between infinite-dimensional function spaces, or operator learning, is essential for many machine learning applications. Although transformer-based operators are popular, they often rely on token-wise attention. These methods treat continuous fields as discrete tokens and usually ignore the global functional structure. We introduce \emph{Functional Attention}, which reinterprets attention as a functional correspondence between adaptive bases. Inspired by geometric functional maps, our method replaces softmax affinities with structured linear operators. This yields a compact, generalizable, resolution-invariant representation that explicitly captures global dependencies. Experiments demonstrate that \emph{Functional Attention} can match state-of-the-art performance in many operator learning tasks, including solving PDEs, 3D segmentation, and regression, while remaining robust to varying discretizations. Project page is available at https://github.com/xjffff/FUNCATTN.

</details>


### [122] [Effective Biological Representation Learning by Masking Gene Expression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31562)
*Kian Kenyon-Dean,Alina Selega,Ihab Bendidi,Jordan M. Sorokin,Luca Bertinetto,David Errington,Hayley Donnella,Oren Kraus*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 TxFM，一种针对 RNA-seq 数据设计的自监督掩码自编码模型。通过精心设计架构和构建 DiverseRNA-1.4M 训练语料库，TxFM 在归纳表征学习评估中优于线性基线及多个大规模转录组基础模型，表明精细化的数据整理与架构设计比单纯扩大数据规模更重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有转录组基础模型在技术噪声和批次效应影响下性能甚至不如线性基线，引发对深度表征学习是否优于直接使用原始转录计数的质疑。本文旨在探索自监督归纳表征学习在转录组学中的可行性。

Method: 提出 TxFM 模型，采用适应多样化 RNA-seq 计数数据的掩码自编码方法；通过消融实验识别关键架构配置以实现强迁移性能；构建 DiverseRNA-1.4M 公共训练语料库进行训练。

Result: TxFM 在 DiverseRNA-1.4M 上训练后，生成的高保真基因表征优于在规模大 100 倍以上的图谱级语料库上训练的基础模型；消融实验确认了关键架构配置对迁移性能的必要性。

Conclusion: 归纳式自监督学习是转录组表征学习的可行方法，前提是精心设计模型架构和训练数据整理策略。

Abstract: RNA sequencing produces rich and diverse datasets of gene expression, offering compelling insights into cellular state and function that have many applications in drug discovery. Modeling such data is challenging due to inherent technical noise and experimental batch effects, as evidenced by many existing transcriptomic foundation models (FMs) underperforming relative to linear baselines. Such results raise the question of whether deep representation learning provides a distinct advantage over the direct use of raw transcript counts. Our work explores this by developing a new self-supervised model, TxFM, with a focus on inductive representation learning evaluations. TxFM employs a masked autoencoding approach tailored to diverse RNA-seq count data, and our ablation study empirically identifies crucial architecture configurations required for strong transfer performance. Additionally, we curate a public training corpus, DiverseRNA-1.4M, and find that TxFM trained on this curated dataset yields high-fidelity gene representations that outperform FMs trained on atlas-scale corpora over 100x larger. Overall, our results indicate that inductive self-supervised learning is a viable modeling approach for transcriptomics representation, provided a careful synthesis of model architecture and training data curation.

</details>


### [123] [Giving Sensors a Voice: Multimodal JEPA for Semantic Time-Series Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31580)
*Utsav Dutta,Gerardo Pastrana,Sina Khoshfetrat Pakazad,Henrik Ohlsson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CHARM是一种结合通道级文本描述和Transformer编码器的多变量时间序列表示学习模型，通过JEPA目标和描述感知门控机制，在异常检测、分类和预测任务中仅用线性探测即取得强性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Transformer架构在语言和视觉领域取得了进展，但面向异构多变量时间序列的通用表示学习仍未充分探索。

Method: 提出CHARM模型，采用通道级文本描述增强Transformer编码器并保持对通道顺序的等变性；训练使用联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）和新型损失函数以促进信息丰富且时间稳定的嵌入；潜空间预测增强对传感器噪声的鲁棒性，描述感知门控通过学习通道间关系提供可解释性。

Result: 在异常检测、分类、短期和长期预测任务中，学到的嵌入仅使用线性探测即达到强性能。

Conclusion: 性能主要来自JEPA目标和条件架构的贡献，文本描述作为通道标识符有助于跨数据集的泛化。

Abstract: Transformer-based architectures have advanced sequence modeling in language and vision, yet general-purpose representation learning for heterogeneous multivariate time series remains underexplored. We introduce CHARM (Channel-Aware Representation Model), which incorporates channel-level textual descriptions into a Transformer encoder equivariant to channel order. CHARM is trained with a Joint Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA) and a novel loss promoting informative, temporally stable embeddings; latent-space prediction encourages robustness to sensor noise while description-aware gating provides interpretability through learned inter-channel relationships. Across anomaly detection, classification, and short- and long-term forecasting, the learned embeddings achieve strong performance using only a linear probe. Performance is driven primarily by the JEPA objective and conditioning architecture, with text descriptions serving as channel identifiers for cross-dataset generalization.

</details>


### [124] [A Tight Theory of Error Feedback Algorithms in Distributed Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31594)
*Daniel Berg Thomsen,Adrien Taylor,Aymeric Dieuleveut*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对分布式学习中的两种主要误差反馈算法（EF和EF21）进行了严格的收敛性分析，通过确定最优步长选择和构造最优Lyapunov函数，获得了与智能体数量无关且恢复单智能体最优保证的收敛结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式学习中通信代价是瓶颈，梯度压缩虽能缓解但会降低收敛保证，误差反馈机制可弥补这一缺陷，然而众多变体的相对性能尚不明确，亟需严格的理论分析。

Method: 对EF和EF21两种主流误差反馈算法进行收敛性分析，通过识别最优步长选择并为每种方法构造最优Lyapunov函数来推导紧的收敛界。

Result: 所得收敛结果与智能体数量无关，且在单智能体场景下恢复了已知的最优保证。

Conclusion: 为EF和EF21算法提供了紧的收敛性分析，明确了其理论性能边界。

Abstract: Communication costs are a major bottleneck in distributed learning and first-order optimization. A common approach to alleviate this issue is to compress the gradient information exchanged between agents. However, such compression typically degrades the convergence guarantees of gradient-based methods. Error feedback mechanisms provide a simple and computationally cheap remedy for this issue, but numerous variants have been proposed, and their relative performance remains poorly understood. This paper provides tight convergence analyses for two of the main error-feedback algorithms from the literature, the classic Error Feedback method (EF) and Error Feedback 21 (EF21), by identifying optimal step-size choices and constructing optimal Lyapunov functions tailored to each method. The results hold independently of the number of agents and recover the known best guarantees possible in the single-agent regime.

</details>


<div id='cs.NE'></div>

# cs.NE [[Back]](#toc)

### [125] [Kernel Foundry: A Diagnosis-driven Evolutionary Kernel Optimizer with Multi-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30359)
*Zixuan Huang,Da Chen,Kecheng Huang,Lihao Yin,Xing Li,Huiling Zhen,Mingxuan Yuan,Zili Shao*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: Kernel Foundry 是一个基于诊断驱动的进化框架，通过专家引导初始化、多岛屿进化搜索和结构化诊断反馈，自动生成正确且高性能的 GPU 内核，在 KernelBench 上达到 Level 2 100% 正确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型在生成 GPU 内核时难以同时保证正确性和硬件感知的高效性，缺乏一种能够自动优化内核并兼顾两者的方法。

Method: 提出 Kernel Foundry 框架，包含：(1) 专家引导的检索增强初始化；(2) 多岛屿进化搜索，迭代使用结构化诊断反馈优化候选内核；(3) 集中式经验库积累可复用的优化知识；(4) 防作弊机制确保内核级计算不被绕过。

Result: 在 KernelBench 上，该方法在正确性和性能上均持续优于强基线方法，Level 2 正确率达到 100%。

Conclusion: 诊断驱动的进化框架能有效结合 LLM 的代码生成能力与结构化优化反馈，在 GPU 内核自动生成中实现正确性与效率的双重提升。

Abstract: Generating high-performance GPU kernels remains challenging due to the need for both correctness and hardware-aware optimization. While large language models (LLMs) show promise in code generation, they often fail to produce kernels that are both correct and efficient.
  We propose Kernel Foundry, a diagnosis-driven evolutionary framework for automatic GPU kernel optimization. Our method combines expert-guided, retrieval-augmented initialization with a multi-island evolutionary search, where candidate kernels are iteratively refined using structured diagnostic feedback. A centralized experience library accumulates reusable optimization knowledge to guide subsequent evolution, while explicit mechanisms prevent cheating behaviors that bypass kernel-level computation.
  Experiments on KernelBench show that our method consistently improves both correctness and performance over strong baselines, achieving up to 100% correctness on Level~2.

</details>


### [126] [From Mean-Field Limits to Semiclassical Concentration: Global Convergence of the Canonical Evolutionary Strategy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30371)
*Matías Neto,Nicolás Garay,Luis Martí,Nayat Sanchez-Pi*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出规范进化策略（CES）作为受控数学框架，通过薛定谔型复制子-突变子的半经典极限分析进化算法的全局收敛性，证明了全局收敛由底层算子的主特征函数控制（几何选择），支持"最平坦者生存"现象，并在高维基准测试中显示出优于共识驱动和梯度方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决随机连续优化中的全局收敛问题，为进化算法的全局收敛提供严格的数学理论基础，避免依赖额外的数值启发式方法。

Method: 将规范进化策略（CES）表述为受控数学框架；通过薛定谔型复制子-突变子方程的半经典极限分析全局收敛；建立从离散个体动力学到确定性平均场极限的严格层级结构；利用几何选择（主特征函数）解释全局收敛机制。

Result: 证明了全局收敛由底层算子的主特征函数控制（几何选择）；CES的复制子-突变子动力学支持内在质量传输，避免了共识驱动方法在全局最小值位于初始支撑外时的过早方差崩溃问题；高维基准（d=30）显示CES在移位初始化场景中实现了更低的残差误差。

Conclusion: 通过将关注点从点态共识转向谱集中，该框架为进化策略的全局收敛提供了稳健的理论基础，无需额外数值启发式方法。

Abstract: We address the issue of global convergence in stochastic continuous optimization. For that purpose, we formulate the Canonical Evolutionary Strategy (CES) as a controlled mathematical framework to analyze global convergence in evolutionary algorithms via the semiclassical limit of a Schr{ö}dinger-type replicator-mutator equation. We provide a rigorous hierarchy from a discrete individual-based dynamics to a deterministic mean-field limit, demonstrating that global convergence is governed by the principal eigenfunction of the underlying operator. This property, defined as Geometric Selection, naturally prioritizes robust, flat optima over narrow local traps, offering a mathematical justification for the ''survival of the flattest'' phenomenon. Moreover, unlike consensus-driven methods that are prone to premature variance collapse when the global minimizer resides outside the initial support, the replicator-mutator dynamics of CES facilitate intrinsic mass transport. High-dimensional benchmarks (d = 30) confirm this advantage, showing that CES achieves lower residual errors in shifted initialization scenarios where standard consensus-driven and gradient-based methods fail to migrate effectively. By shifting the focus from point-wise consensus to spectral concentration, our framework provides a robust theoretical foundation for global convergence in Evolution Strategies (ES) without the need for additional numerical heuristics.

</details>


### [127] [GP-GOMEA with GPU-Based Fitness Evaluations: Design and Performance Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30954)
*Jasper Post,Johannes Koch,Anton Bouter,Tanja Alderliesten,Peter A. N. Bosman*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文首次实现了GP-GOMEA遗传编程算法的完全GPU加速适应度评估，通过设计GPU友好的个体表示和评估策略，大幅提升了评估吞吐量，使得在相同时间内可进行数量级更多的评估，从而在标准符号回归基准上取得更好性能，并首次实现问题无关的进化算法在4小时内可靠回归最大的费曼方程之一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GP-GOMEA虽能发现小型可解释模型，但计算成本高昂，限制了其在大数据集和复杂目标表达式上的应用。为突破这一瓶颈，作者借助GPU的大规模并行能力，首次将其适应度评估阶段完全迁移至GPU。

Method: 设计GPU友好的模板个体表示与评估策略，充分利用种群搜索的固有并行性，实现适应度评估的GPU加速。

Result: 评估吞吐量大幅提升，在四个标准符号回归基准上表现更优（尤其对大数据集和大种群），并能高效评估此前不可行的大数据集和复杂模板；首次实现问题无关进化算法在4小时内可靠回归最大费曼方程之一。

Conclusion: GPU加速显著提升了GP-GOMEA的扩展能力和实用性，使大规模符号回归分析成为可能，并揭示了表达式结构对搜索难度的影响。

Abstract: GP-GOMEA is a state-of-the-art evolutionary algorithm for symbolic regression, known for discovering small and interpretable models. However, its computational cost remains substantial, limiting its applicability to larger datasets and more complex target expressions. In contrast, the rise of modern subsymbolic approaches, particularly deep learning, has been driven largely by the massive parallelism offered by GPUs. In this work, we take the first major step toward a fully GPU-accelerated GP-GOMEA by introducing a GPU-based fitness evaluation scheme. We design a GPU-friendly representation of GP-GOMEA's template-based individuals and a corresponding evaluation strategy that exploits the inherent parallelism of population-based search. This substantially increases evaluation throughput, enabling orders of magnitude more evaluations within the same time budget. Across four standard symbolic regression benchmarks, this increased evaluation capacity yields performance improvements, particularly for larger datasets and larger population sizes. Moreover, the ability to efficiently evaluate much larger datasets and more complex templates enables analyses that were previously infeasible, allowing us to systematically analyze what makes expressions increasingly difficult for GP-GOMEA, providing new insights into how expression structure affects search difficulty. Finally, for the first time, this expanded capability allows a problem-agnostic evolutionary algorithm to reliably regress one of the largest Feynman equations within four hours.

</details>


### [128] [Linear Ordering Problem: Time for a Change](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31051)
*Fabrizio Fagiolo,Marco Baioletti,Valentino Santucci*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文针对线性排序问题（LOP），提出了基于最新经济数据的新基准测试集，并设计了一种利用元启发式算法生成多样化高质量解的方案，同时引入评估解质量和多样性的指标，在传统单解和新提出的多解场景下进行了实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LOP算法多基于过时的宏观经济数据基准进行评估，无法反映当代经济结构；且LOP实例通常存在多个差异显著的全局最优解，依赖单一解的应用场景面临挑战。

Method: 构建基于最新真实经济数据的基准测试集；设计利用先进LOP元启发式算法的方案以生成多样化高质量解集合；提出同时评估解质量和多样性的指标。

Result: 在传统单解设置和新引入的多解场景下，对所提基准测试集进行了实验并报告了结果。

Conclusion: 新基准更贴合当代经济结构，多解方案可有效应对LOP多最优解问题，为实际应用提供更可靠的解集。

Abstract: The Linear Ordering Problem (LOP) is a fundamental combinatorial optimization problem with important applications in areas such as economics, social choice, and machine learning. Its most prominent use is the triangulation of economic input-output tables, which helps identify critical industries in an economy. Most existing algorithms have been evaluated on benchmarks derived from outdated macroeconomic data, which no longer reflect the structure of contemporary economies. Furthermore, LOP instances often exhibit many distinct global optima that can differ substantially from one another, creating challenges for applications that rely on a single solution. To address these limitations, we introduce a novel benchmark suite derived from up-to-date real-world economic data and an algorithmic scheme that leverages state-of-the-art LOP metaheuristics to generate diverse sets of high-quality solutions, together with metrics for assessing both quality and diversity. Experiments were conducted to report results on the proposed benchmark suite under both the traditional single-solution setting and the newly introduced multi-solution scenario

</details>


### [129] [Developing a novel Comorbidities Index for predicting 10-year mortality in Prostate Cancer patients: A computational data-driven approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31213)
*Davide Farinati,Francesco Barletta,Paolo Zaurito,Simone Scuderi,Nicholas Raison,Alejandro Granados,Prokar Dasgupta,Giorgio Gandaglia,Alberto Briganti*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出一个数据驱动框架，利用群体生物启发算法（PBBIAs）重新校准查尔森合并症指数（CCI）的权重，并为接受根治性前列腺切除术（RP）的前列腺癌（PCa）患者推导出更优的合并症指数，以提升十年其他原因死亡率预测的准确性。实验表明，GA、FST-PSO、SLIM等方法在区分度上优于原始CCI和PCCI，最高将C-index提升约0.1，GPLearn则兼具紧凑性和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始CCI权重可能无法反映当代预后，而在PCa患者中，准确估计十年其他原因死亡率对于避免过度治疗（仅对预期寿命≥10年的患者推荐根治性治疗）至关重要。现有指数在RP候选人群中的预测精度不足。

Method: 基于单中心回顾性队列，应用群体生物启发算法（PBBIAs），包括遗传编程（GP）、遗传算法（GA）、FST-PSO、SLIM等六种优化策略，重新校准合并症权重并演化替代符号公式，优化目标是十年生存区分度。

Result: GA、FST-PSO和SLIM优于原始CCI和PCCI，尤其在使用PCa特异性变量时，C-index提升高达0.1。GPLearn在保持竞争性能的同时提供紧凑可解释的模型。

Conclusion: 该方法为RP患者选择提供了一个更新且可解释的工具，能够更准确地评估合并症负担，改善患者筛选决策，平衡肿瘤获益与竞争死亡风险。

Abstract: The Charlson Comorbidities Index (CCI) is a weighted additive index widely used to estimate ten-year mortality risk, but its original weights may not reflect contemporary prognoses. This limitation is critical in Prostate Cancer (PCa), where radical treatment is recommended only for patients with a life expectancy of at least ten years. For candidates eligible for Radical Prostatectomy (RP), accurate estimation of ten-year other-cause mortality is essential to balance oncological benefit against competing risks and avoid overtreatment. We propose a data-driven framework to derive a comorbidity index tailored to PCa patients considered for RP. Using a retrospective single-institution cohort, we apply Population-Based Bio-Inspired Algorithms (PBBIAs) to recalibrate comorbidity weights and evolve alternative symbolic formulations optimized for ten-year survival discrimination. We compared six optimization strategies, including symbolic regression approaches based on Genetic Programming (GP), population-based metaheuristics, clinically validated baselines, and survival prediction models. Results show that GA, FST-PSO, and SLIM outperform both the original CCI and the PCCI, particularly when PCa-specific variables are included, improving the Concordance Index by up to 0.1. GPLearn yields compact and interpretable models with competitive performance. Overall, the proposed approach provides an updated and interpretable tool to improve patient selection for RP.

</details>
